### [1] [C-Norm: Cell-Distribution Normalization Enables Precision Recognition of Medical-Cell Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13116)
*Yang Qianl,Liu Xiany,Dai Daw,Chen Jing,Shen Xiaoj,Fu Kaiw,Tang Ming,Zou Dongl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于ThinPrep细胞学测试（TCT）图像分析的Cell-Distribution Normalization（C-Norm）方法，结合YOLOv12和DINOv3框架，解决了TCT图像中细胞群体空间分布不均和高质量标注数据有限的问题，实现了最先进的细胞检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TCT虽能实现宫颈癌早期筛查，但人工阅片耗时且诊断结果存在不一致性。现有AI检测模型在真实临床条件下性能不佳，主要受限于两个关键约束：TCT切片中细胞群体空间分布不均，以及依赖专业病理学家标注的高质量细胞学数据有限。

Method: 提出了Cell-Distribution Normalization（C-Norm）方法：1. 从原始TCT图像中解耦异常细胞和正常细胞；2. 重新合成这些细胞以确保细胞群体均匀分布，从而减轻分布偏差导致的泛化性能下降。在此基础上，将YOLOv12框架与DINOv3模块集成，利用YOLO模型的高级检测能力和DINOv3的优越特征表示能力，捕获TCT图像精确识别所需的关键形态细微差异。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提方法实现了最先进的性能，显著优于主流检测算法。

Conclusion: 提出的C-Norm方法结合YOLOv12和DINOv3的混合架构，有效解决了TCT图像分析中的细胞分布不均和数据限制问题，为TCT图像检测提供了有效的解决方案，在宫颈癌筛查的AI辅助诊断中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: ThinPrep Cytologic Test (TCT) enables early cervical cancer screening, but manual reading is time-consuming and yields inconsistent diagnostic results among cytopathologists. Existing AI detection models perform poorly under real clinical conditions, primarily restricted by two key constraints: unbalanced spatial distribution of cell populations in TCT slides, and limited high-quality annotated cytology data relying on professional pathologist labeling. To address these limitations, we propose a Cell-Distribution Normalization (C-Norm) method. By decoupling abnormal and normal cells from the original TCT images and re-synthesizing them, this method ensures a uniform distribution of cell populations, thereby mitigating generalization degradation caused by distribution bias. Building upon this, we integrate the YOLOv12 framework with a DINOv3 module. This hybrid architecture leverages the advanced detection capability of YOLO models and the superior feature representations of DINOv3 to capture subtle morphological nuances essential for precise recognition of TCT images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance, significantly outperforming mainstream detection algorithms. The complete implementation is available at: https://github.com/ddw2AIGROUP2CQUPT/Cell-Norm

</details>


### [2] [Boogu-Image-0.1: Boosting Open-Source Unified Multimodal Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13125)
*Guoxuan Chen,Chufeng Xiao,Haoran Yang,Siyue Xie,Binxiao Huang,Ming Zhang,Cheuk Him Chau,Xinyu Fu,Yingzhao Lian,Tom S. Y. Li,Jintao Lin,Bowen Dong,Zian Qian,Yuhao Liu,Yuxuan Hu,Weikang Shi,Bin Zou,Bowen Zheng,Haoxuan Che,Chang Chen,Yuyang He,Heyang Sun,Tianyu Huang,Chong Hou Choi,Cheng Gong,Han Shi,Haoli Bai,Xihui Liu,Hongsheng Li,Qifeng Chen,Chao Huang,Rui Liu,Chenyang Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Boogu-Image-0.1是一个开源的多模态理解和生成模型家族，包括Base、Turbo、Edit和Edit-Turbo变体，在文本到图像生成、快速推理、指令编辑和双语文本渲染方面具有竞争力，训练成本仅约40万美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前闭源多模态系统（如Nano-Banana-Pro和GPT-Image-2）通过系统级集成获得强大性能，但其内部实践大多未公开。研究目标是开发开源替代方案，展示通过针对性改进和有限计算预算仍能达到接近闭源系统的性能。

Method: 采用针对性改进：1）模型理解能力提升；2）数据质量优化；3）训练流程改进；4）代理推理时间缩放技术。使用仅有2.086亿张独特图像的数据集，基模型理论训练成本约40万美元。

Result: 全面评估显示Boogu-Image-0.1在标准基准测试中一致匹配或超越其他开源模型，并接近领先闭源系统的结果。模型在高质量文本到图像生成、快速推理、指令编辑和双语文本渲染方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 研究表明通过针对性改进模型理解、数据质量和训练流程，加上代理推理时间缩放，即使在有限计算预算下也能显著提升生成和编辑性能。模型在Apache 2.0许可下开源权重、代码和配方，推动开放生态系统发展。

Abstract: We introduce Boogu-Image-0.1, an open-source unified multimodal understanding and generation model family, comprising Base, Turbo, Edit, and Edit-Turbo variants. It delivers competitive performance in high-quality text-to-image generation, fast inference, instruction-based editing, and bilingual (Chinese-English) text rendering. Closed-source multimodal systems like Nano-Banana-Pro and GPT-Image-2 achieve strong performance through system-level integration rather than a single model, yet their internal practices remain largely undisclosed. In this work, we demonstrate that targeted improvements in model understanding, data quality, and training pipelines, coupled with agentic inference-time scaling, can substantially enhance generation and editing performance even under highly constrained compute budgets. Comprehensive evaluations show that Boogu-Image-0.1 consistently matches or surpasses other open-source models across standard benchmarks, and achieves results approaching leading closed-source systems. Notably, this is accomplished with only 208.62 million unique images. The base model's theoretical training cost is only approximately \$400K. We share practical discussions that we believe are valuable to the broader research community, and release weights, code, and recipes under Apache 2.0 to advance the open ecosystem for unified multimodal understanding and generation. Our code is available here: https://github.com/Boogu-Project/Boogu-Image.

</details>


### [3] [A Masked Autoencoder Approach to Unsupervised Steel Surface Defect Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13178)
*Shrey Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 使用基于Transformer的掩码自编码器进行钢铁表面缺陷的无监督表征学习，通过掩码重建和辅助定位任务预训练编码器，最终通过聚类实现91.3%的缺陷类别匹配精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钢铁表面缺陷自动化视觉检测中，带标签缺陷数据稀缺且获取成本高，而无标签表面图像丰富，这促使研究无需类标签就能学习有用表征的自监督方法。

Method: 采用基于Transformer的掩码自编码器：预训练阶段随机掩码75%的输入图像块，使用轻量级解码器从可见的25%区域重建掩码区域；编码器同时训练辅助缺陷定位目标（仅作为训练信号，不用于检测评估）。随后使用UMAP进行降维，并通过凝聚聚类对预训练编码器提取的特征进行聚类。

Result: 解码器达到0.92的结构相似性分数和0.47的均方误差；预训练编码器特征经聚类后，针对六种已知缺陷类别获得91.3%的匈牙利匹配准确率。

Conclusion: 掩码自编码器在钢铁表面缺陷的无监督表征学习中表现有效，能够从少量可见信息中学习有意义的特征，结合聚类方法可实现高精度的缺陷类别分组，为缺陷检测提供了实用的无监督解决方案。

Abstract: Automated visual inspection of steel surface defects is a recurring quality control task in which labeled defect data is scarce and costly to obtain, while unlabeled surface images are abundant, which motivates self supervised methods that learn useful representations without class labels. A Transformer based Masked Autoencoder is used here to learn representations of steel surface defects for unsupervised grouping. During pretraining, 75% of the input image patches are randomly masked, and a lightweight decoder reconstructs the masked regions from the visible 25%. The encoder is trained jointly with an auxiliary defect localization objective, used only as a training signal and not evaluated as a detector. The decoder reaches a structural similarity score of 0.92 and a mean squared error of 0.47. Features from the pretrained encoder are then clustered using UMAP for dimensionality reduction and Agglomerative clustering, reaching a Hungarian matched accuracy of 91.3% against the six known defect categories.

</details>


### [4] [MGFace: Mask-Gated Face Matching via Conditional Similarity Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13187)
*Huy Che,Hoang-Minh Trinh,Dinh-Duy Phan,Duc-Lung Vu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MGFace是一个针对口罩遮挡人脸的识别系统，它通过预测查询人脸是否戴口罩，智能选择匹配策略：对无口罩人脸使用全局嵌入匹配，对戴口罩人脸才激活细粒度局部重排序，从而在保持高识别准确率的同时显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人脸识别系统在正常条件下表现良好，但在人脸被遮挡（特别是口罩）时性能显著下降。现有重排序方法通过利用局部相似性提高鲁棒性，但这些方法依赖于计算成本高的细粒度匹配机制，限制了在大规模检索场景中的效率。

Method: MGFace采用口罩门控的人脸识别流程：1）预测查询人脸的口罩状态；2）根据状态条件性地路由相似度计算；3）对无口罩查询使用全局嵌入匹配；4）仅对戴口罩查询激活口罩感知的局部重排序；5）专注于可靠的上半脸区域，避免不必要的细粒度计算。

Result: 在扩展的LFW-Mask数据集上，MGFace在FaceNet骨干网络上达到超过80%的识别准确率，在ArcFace骨干网络上超过90%。相比之前的基于EMD的重排序方法，MGFace获得了更好的识别性能，同时将查询时间减少了约20倍。

Conclusion: MGFace有效地提高了戴口罩人脸的识别准确率，同时保持低计算开销。该系统通过智能选择匹配策略，为遮挡人脸识别提供了一种高效解决方案，适合大规模检索应用。

Abstract: Face identification has achieved remarkable performance under normal conditions. Yet, its accuracy often degrades significantly when query faces are partially occluded, especially by facial masks. Existing re-ranking approaches improve robustness by exploiting patch-level similarities. Still, they often rely on costly, fine-grained matching mechanisms, which limit their efficiency in large-scale retrieval scenarios. In this paper, we propose MGFace, a mask-gated face identification pipeline that predicts the mask status of a query face and conditionally routes the similarity computation accordingly. Specifically, MGFace distinguishes between masked and unmasked queries, applies global embedding matching to unmasked queries, and activates mask-aware patch-level re-ranking only for masked queries. This design focuses on reliable upper-face regions while avoiding unnecessary fine-grained computation. Experiments on the extended LFW-Mask dataset show that MGFace achieves over 80% identification accuracy with the FaceNet backbone and over 90% with the ArcFace backbone. Compared with a previous EMD-based re-ranking method, MGFace achieves better identification performance while reducing query time by approximately 20x. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of MGFace in improving masked-face identification accuracy with low computational overhead. The source code is available at https://github.com/chequanghuy/MGFace.

</details>


### [5] [Self-Supervised Visual Representation Learning: Pretrain-Finetuning or Joint Training?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13192)
*Nusrat Munia,Tyler Ward,Nishat Nayla,Matthew A. Massey,Abdullah-Al-Zubaer Imran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了自监督学习的两种训练范式：预训练后微调(PFT)与联合训练(JT)，发现在不同任务、标签数据量和领域复杂度下两种方法各有优劣，并为混合自监督半监督学习建立了实证基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习主要采用两阶段方法（无标签数据预训练 + 有标签数据微调），虽然效果显著，但自监督与监督目标之间的相互作用尚未被充分理解。本文旨在系统研究联合优化这两种目标是否提供更好的替代方案。

Method: 系统地比较了两种训练范式：(1)预训练后微调(PFT)：先在无标签数据上预训练，然后在有标签数据上微调；(2)联合训练(JT)：在同一网络中同时优化自监督和监督损失。在八个代表性SSL方法上，针对自然、医学、危机响应和遥感数据的多样化计算机视觉任务，评估了不同标签数据比例下的性能。

Result: 研究发现PFT和JT的相对有效性高度依赖于任务特性、标签数据的可用性和领域复杂度。JT在提高数据和训练效率方面表现一致，且在低标签设置下更稳健；而PFT在更专门的领域中更可靠。研究还分析了表示质量、鲁棒性和跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 为混合SSL基半监督学习建立了全面的实证基准，提供了关于自监督和监督目标在优化过程中如何相互作用的新见解，并为跨多样化视觉应用选择适当训练策略提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Self-supervision is a powerful technique for learning visual representations from unlabeled data. Existing techniques primarily adopt a two-stage approach for self-supervised learning (SSL): a pretraining stage on unlabeled data followed by a finetuning stage on labeled data. While this pipeline has demonstrated extreme effectiveness, the interaction between self-supervised and supervised learning objectives remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we systematically investigate whether jointly optimizing the self-supervised and supervised objectives during training provides a better alternative. We compare two training paradigms: (1) the aforementioned pretraining followed by finetuning (PFT) and (2) joint training (JT), where self-supervised and supervised losses are optimized simultaneously in the same network. Across eight representative SSL methods and diverse computer vision tasks on natural, medical, crisis response, and remote sensing data, we evaluate performance under varying percentages of labeled data. Our results reveal that the relative effectiveness of PFT and JT depends strongly on the task at hand, the availability of labeled data, and the complexity of the domain. We find that JT consistently improves data and training efficiency while being robust in low-label settings, while PFT is more reliable in more specialized domains. We further analyze representation quality, robustness, and cross-domain generalization, providing new insights into how self-supervised and supervised objectives interact during optimization. We establish a comprehensive empirical benchmark for hybrid SSL-based semi-supervised learning and offer practical guidance for selecting appropriate training strategies across diverse vision applications.

</details>


### [6] [Continuously Evolving Deepfake Detection: An Architecture and Public-Benchmark Evaluation of a Dynamic Detection System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13234)
*Ken Jon Miyachi,Dylan Uys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BitMind Forensics (BMF)是一个通过Bittensor SN34对抗性竞赛持续训练的深度伪造检测系统，在真实世界内容上表现出显著优于静态检测器的性能，在多个公共数据集上达到高AUC分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度伪造检测器在学术基准上表现优异，但在真实世界内容上性能严重下降（AUC下降45-50%）。这是由于静态检测器针对不断发展的生成技术而训练，存在结构性缺陷。

Method: 提出BitMind Forensics (BMF)，通过Bittensor SN34开放对抗性竞赛进行训练，持续刷新训练分布。系统包含图像、通用视频和人类视频检查点，在19个公共数据集上评估，包括传统人脸交换套件和最新的野外及AI生成媒体基准。

Result: BMF在多个基准测试中表现卓越：在Sumsub图像上达到0.936 AUC，四条件操纵测试集上0.872 AUC；在Deepfake-Eval-2024上与最佳商业检测器相当或超越；在21生成器AI图像面板上达到0.991 AUC；在DFDC和Celeb-DF v2上显著优于FF++训练的前沿模型；时间研究表明连续导出版本在未见生成器上持续改进。

Conclusion: 通过开放对抗性竞赛持续训练的方法能有效解决深度伪造检测中静态模型与动态生成前沿之间的结构性差距，显著提升在真实世界内容上的检测性能，为深度伪造检测提供了一种更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors that achieve near-perfect scores on academic benchmarks collapse on real-world content: recent in-the-wild evaluations report AUC drops of 45-50% for state-of-the-art open-source models. We argue this gap is structural: static detectors are trained once against a moving generative frontier. We present BitMind Forensics (BMF), trained through Bittensor SN34, an open adversarial competition that continually refreshes the training distribution. We evaluate one dated export comprising image, general-video, and human-video checkpoints across nineteen public datasets: the canonical face-swap suites (FaceForensics++, Celeb-DF v1/v2/++, DFDC, DFD, UADFV, DF40) and recent in-the-wild and AI-generated-media benchmarks (Sumsub, Deepfake-Eval-2024, WildRF, Community Forensics, AIGCDetectBench, GenImage, AI-GenBench, AIGIBench, RAID, GenVidBench, GenVideo-100K). BMF reaches 0.936 AUC on Sumsub's original images and 0.872 pooled AUC over its full four-condition manipulation battery (1.4M images), staying robust under perturbation (0.855 JPEG, 0.799 downscaled), while GPEN enhancement improves detection (0.996). On Deepfake-Eval-2024, it matches the best commercial detector on images (0.915 vs 0.90) and exceeds it on video (0.822 vs 0.79), far above the best open-source detectors (0.56 and 0.63). It reaches 0.991 AUC on a 21-generator AI-image panel and 0.918 on GenVidBench, and exceeds the FF++-trained frontier on DFDC (0.947 vs 0.843) and Celeb-DF v2 (0.9985 vs 0.956), both contamination-audited, with statistical parity on Celeb-DF++. In a temporal study, successive dated exports improve on held-out media from generators absent from the static baseline's training (image 0.842 to 0.902; video 0.864 to 0.936). Our evaluation harness is public, and at publication the production API serves the exact evaluated snapshot for independent verification.

</details>


### [7] [Active Learning for Efficient Annotation of Surgical Videos with Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13237)
*Manasa Dendukuri,Matjaz Jogan,Daniel A. Hashimoto,Guiqiu Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合主动学习与双损失优化的人机协同知识获取框架，显著降低了手术视频中工具定位和分割的标注工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹腔镜视频的精确时空标注耗时且需要专业知识，而构建大规模全标注数据集成本高昂，阻碍了手术工具分割模型的可扩展发展和临床应用。

Method: 采用基础模型从视频生成时间一致的类别激活图，结合弱监督损失（基于视频级工具存在标签）和图像级掩码损失（基于主动学习获得的人工修正标注）。通过迭代生成伪掩码引导专家细化模型知识，实现人机协同的渐进式标注。

Result: 与传统全手动标注相比，该框架在训练结束时将手术视频标注工作量减少了50%，无需初始大规模全标注数据集即可扩展手术工具分割模型的开发。

Conclusion: 该人机协同迭代精炼框架通过最小化专家输入实现高效知识获取，为将工具分割扩展到更大型、更多样化数据集和真实临床场景提供了实用且可部署的策略。

Abstract: Precise spatial-temporal annotation of laparoscopic videos is time-consuming and requires expert knowledge. We propose a human-in-the-loop knowledge acquisition framework that combines active learning with dual-loss optimization to significantly reduce the annotation effort needed for automatic localization and segmentation of objects in the surgical field. Our method employs a foundation model to generate temporally consistent class activation maps (CAMs) from video using two complementary training objectives: a weak supervision loss on video-level tool presence labels for weakly annotated data, and an image-level mask loss on human-corrected annotations obtained through active learning. Rather than requiring dense pixel-level annotation upfront, our pipeline iteratively proposes pseudo-masks that guide the expert annotator to refine the knowledge previously captured by the model. We demonstrate that our framework reduces the effort of surgical video annotation by 50% by the end of training in comparison to fully manual annotation. Through eliminating the need for large, fully annotated datasets from the start, this framework enables scalability to the development of surgical tool segmentation models. This iterative human-in-the-loop refinement supports efficient knowledge acquisition with minimal expert input, providing a practical and deployable strategy for expanding tool segmentation to larger, more diverse datasets and real-world clinical settings.

</details>


### [8] [Just-In-Time Scene Graph Growth: Combating Perceptual Saturation in Long-Horizon Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13245)
*Yue Chang,Rufeng Chen,Yifan Tian,Dazhi Huang,Zhaofan Zhang,Yi Chen,Wenze Zhang,Li Chen,Hui Xiong,Sihong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JITOMA是一个面向边缘平台的即时按需内存激活框架，通过任务热图过滤观测、LLM解析意图并动态唤醒局部锚点，实现3D场景图的高效构建，减少感知饱和与资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D场景图（3DSG）构建采用“先建后滤”的离线流水线，导致严重的观测冗余与感知饱和效应，无法满足边缘平台对实时性与低延迟的需求。

Method: JITOMA采用前后端分离的闭环框架：前端利用任务热图对连续观测进行过滤，仅维护低成本、休眠的全局锚点基础；后端通过大型语言模型解析机器人意图，动态唤醒任务相关的局部锚点，触发密集节点描述与功能推断等资源密集型操作，实现按需激活的局部子图处理。

Result: 实验表明，JITOMA显著减少了活跃图规模与描述延迟，并在长时程任务切换下保持稳定的处理时间；JITOMA-Bench评估套件验证了其在多任务与复杂多步推理中的动态能力与感知饱和权衡优势。

Conclusion: JITOMA框架通过将任务推理、感知与记忆统一为按需激活的即时增长过程，有效解决了传统3D场景图构建中因全场景映射导致的感知饱和与资源浪费问题，为边缘平台上的具身智能体提供了低延迟、高效率的感知-记忆协同方案。

Abstract: While 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) provide crucial structured representations for embodied agents, conventional Ahead-of-Time, build-everything-then-filter pipelines conflict with the real-time, low-latency demands of edge platforms, inducing a perceptual saturation effect via severe observation redundancy. To resolve this, we present JITOMA (Just-In-Time On-demand Memory Activation), a closed-loop framework that unifies task reasoning, perception, and memory into a just-in-time growth process. Instead of exhaustively mapping the entire environment, JITOMA leverages a top-down task heatmap at the frontend to filter continuous observations, routing minimal streams to maintain a global foundation of low-cost, dormant anchors. Upon a cognitive query, the backend Large Language Model (LLM) parses the robotic intent to dynamically awaken task-relevant anchors, triggering resource-intensive operations -- such as dense node captioning and functional inference -- exclusively within the activated local subgraph. To evaluate these dynamic capabilities and study perceptual saturation trade-offs, we introduce JITOMA-Bench, a comprehensive suite for long-horizon multi-tasking and complex multi-step reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that JITOMA substantially reduces active graph size and captioning latency, while maintaining stable processing time under long-horizon task switching.

</details>


### [9] [AffectFlow-DINO: Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Task Affect Estimation via Conditional Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13250)
*Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Abdellah Zakaria Sellam,Fadi Dornaika,Abdenour Hadid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AffectFlow-DINO系统，这是一个基于DINOv3视觉Transformer的多任务学习模型，通过条件整流流解码器处理面部表情的模糊性，实现了包含效价-唤醒度、表情分类和动作单元的多种面部行为分析任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中面部行为固有的模糊性使得单一的确定性预测不足以准确捕捉复杂的面部表情变化。传统模型无法处理这种歧义性，因此需要能够生成不确定性感知的一对多预测系统。

Method: 1. 基于冻结的DINOv3 ViT-S/16主干网络
2. 引入条件整流流解码器头，替代传统确定性预测
3. 通过蒙特卡洛采样实现一对多预测
4. 联合处理三个任务：连续效价-唤醒度估计、八类表情分类、十二个动作单元检测
5. 采用后验阈值校准处理类别不平衡问题

Result: 1. 整流流解码显著改进确定性预测，特别是效价-唤醒度估计（CCC-V +0.058）
2. 后验阈值校准有效恢复严重不平衡稀有类别的性能（如恐惧类：3.8% → 33.1%）
3. 结合主干微调和流重调，最终模型达到P_MTL=1.177，远超官方基准线的0.45
4. 通过广泛消融研究验证了各组件有效性

Conclusion: AffectFlow-DINO系统成功解决了面部行为分析中的模糊性问题，通过条件生成建模和不确定性感知预测，在多任务学习中取得了显著性能提升，为第11届ABAW挑战赛提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We present \textbf{AffectFlow-DINO}, a multi-task learning system for the 11th ABAW challenge that extends a standard deterministic architecture with a conditional rectified-flow head to model the inherent ambiguity of in-the-wild facial behavior. Instead of predicting a single affect estimate, the model learns a conditional generative distribution, enabling uncertainty-aware one-to-many predictions through Monte Carlo sampling. The system jointly estimates continuous valence-arousal, classifies eight facial expressions, and detects twelve Action Units from static face images. Built on a frozen DINOv3 ViT-S/16 backbone, extensive ablation studies show that rectified-flow decoding consistently improves deterministic prediction, particularly for valence-arousal estimation (CCC-V $+0.058$). We further show that post-hoc threshold calibration effectively recovers performance on severely imbalanced rare classes (e.g., Fear: $3.8\% \rightarrow 33.1\%$) without retraining. Combined with backbone fine-tuning and flow retuning, the final model achieves $\mathbf{P_{MTL}=1.177}$, substantially outperforming the official challenge baseline of $P_{MTL}=0.45$.

</details>


### [10] [Differentiable Polarized Path Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13265)
*Pramod Rao,Jérémy Riviere,Xilong Zhou,Abhijeet Ghosh,Abhimitra Meka,Thabo Beeler,Marc Habermann,Christian Theobalt,Delio Vicini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种鲁棒的偏振感知可微分路径追踪方法，解决了偏振可微分渲染中的数值不稳定性问题，实现了材料和照明参数的高效稳定优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理可微分渲染方法主要基于辐射强度，忽略了偏振线索对场景几何和材质属性的约束作用。偏振光的正向模拟虽然可通过Mueller-Stokes计算实现，但反向模式微分在此领域面临重大挑战，特别是常见偏振算子（如线性偏振器和漫反射）的秩亏缺特性违反了标准梯度估计器的可逆性假设，导致数值不稳定。

Method: 提出了一种鲁棒的偏振感知可微分路径追踪方法，通过结合路径回放和本地缓存的策略来估计无偏梯度。该方法扩展了传统可微分渲染技术，专门处理偏振算子的秩亏缺特性，确保在复杂场景中稳定优化。

Result: 该方法能够高效稳定地优化复杂场景中的材料和照明参数，扩展了基于物理的反向渲染的应用范围。通过解决偏振可微分渲染中的数值不稳定性问题，实现了对偏振线索的有效利用。

Conclusion: 这项工作提出了一种稳健的偏振感知可微分路径追踪框架，解决了偏振可微分渲染中的核心挑战，为利用偏振线索进行更准确的逆向渲染问题（如3D重建、反射率估计和光照估计）提供了有效的工具。

Abstract: Physically based differentiable rendering has proven to be a powerful tool for inverse rendering problems (e.g., 3D reconstruction, reflectance estimation, lighting estimation). However, most existing methods operate solely on radiometric intensity, discarding valuable polarization cues that constrain scene geometry and material properties. While forward simulation of polarized light is well-defined via Mueller-Stokes calculus, extending reverse-mode differentiation to this domain presents significant challenges. The rank-deficient nature of common polarimetric operators, such as linear polarizers and diffuse reflections, violates the invertibility assumptions of standard gradient estimators like path replay backpropagation and results in numerical instability. We address this by proposing a robust, polarization-aware differentiable path tracing method. Our approach estimates unbiased gradients through a combination of path replay and local caching. This formulation enables efficient and stable optimization of material and lighting parameters in complex scenes, broadening the applicability of physically based inverse rendering. Project page: https://vcai.mpi-inf.mpg.de/projects/DPPT/

</details>


### [11] [FOLIO: Focused Semantic Memory for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13298)
*Haoyang Fan,Dhruv Parikh,Anvitha Ramachandran,Sameh Gobriel,Nilesh Jain,Rajgopal Kannan,Viktor Prasanna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FOLIO是一个无需训练、专注于语义记忆的在线流视频理解系统，通过动态焦点状态选择性地保留流中的重要部分为高细节，同时紧凑存储周围上下文，实现了高效的历史信息组织与检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线流视频理解中，视频帧持续增长且查询时间未知，传统方法面临内存无限增长和保留冗余视觉细节的问题，而后来的查询通常依赖于特定实体、动作及其时间变化，需要一个能选择性地保留重要信息并组织历史记忆的系统。

Method: FOLIO采用无需训练的方法，在段级别更新记忆，由动态焦点状态引导，结合短期视觉缓冲区和围绕观测实体组织的长期语义记忆，并与视觉证据缓存链接。查询时使用轻量级混合检索，结合结构化记忆的直接匹配和语义查询扩展。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B上达到82.0/69.1的Perception/Backward准确率（OVO-Bench），在StreamingBench上达到74.5的整体准确率，同时通过为焦点实体保留详细记录并紧凑存储周围上下文，显著降低了维护流记忆的成本。

Conclusion: FOLIO通过在语义级别组织和更新记忆，选择性地为重要实体保留高细节记录，同时紧凑存储上下文，解决了在线流视频理解中的历史信息组织挑战，实现了最先进的性能与高效内存利用的平衡。

Abstract: In online streaming video understanding, a video stream continues to arrive and queries may be issued at any time. Because streaming frames grow without bound, the system must continuously compress and retain information from the observed video prefix while future frames and future queries remain unknown. The core challenge is deciding what information to retain and how to organize the maintained history: as this history grows with the stream, memory cost increases and many redundant visual details are retained, whereas later queries often depend on specific entities, actions, and their temporal changes. To address this challenge, we introduce FOLIO, a training-free focused semantic memory system that records important parts of the stream in higher detail while keeping surrounding context compact. As the stream arrives, FOLIO updates memory at the segment level, guided by a dynamic focus state, combining a short-term visual buffer with a long-term semantic memory organized around observed entities and linked to a visual-evidence cache. At query time, lightweight hybrid retrieval combines direct matching over the structured memory with semantic query expansion. FOLIO achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching 82.0/69.1 Perception/Backward accuracy on OVO-Bench with Qwen3-VL-8B and 74.5 overall accuracy on StreamingBench, while substantially reducing the cost of maintaining streaming memory by reserving detailed records for focused entities and storing surrounding context compactly.

</details>


### [12] [Improving Medical Image Generative Models with Fréchet Distance Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13300)
*Andrew Marshall,Xuanang Xu,Xiaoran Zhang,Rui Wang,Lawrence Staib,James Duncan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出用Fréchet距离损失（FD-loss）微调扩散生成模型，以更好地捕捉肿瘤的复杂形态特征，提升合成医学图像质量，进而显著改善下游肿瘤分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成模型在合成医学图像方面展现出巨大潜力，但标准去噪目标（最小化逐像素误差）往往会平滑肿瘤特有的高方差不规则结构，导致模型难以捕捉异质性肿瘤的复杂形态特征，从而限制了其在下游临床任务（如分割）中的实用性。

Method: 提出使用Fréchet距离损失（FD-loss）对扩散生成模型进行微调。FD-loss通过在预训练编码器空间中对齐真实图像与生成图像的一阶和二阶特征统计量，鼓励生成器捕捉异质性肿瘤的复杂结构变化。该方法在多种架构设置（包括自然图像和医学图像编码器）以及多个肝脏和脑癌数据集（涵盖CT和MRI模态）上进行了集成验证。

Result: 使用FD-loss正则化的合成数据训练的下游分割网络 consistently 实现更优性能，与未正则化的合成数据增强相比，肿瘤DSC提升超过5%。定性分析表明，这些性能提升与更真实的肿瘤合成和更少的分割幻觉相关。

Conclusion: FD-loss作为一种有效的正则化方法，能显著提升医学图像生成模型在捕捉肿瘤复杂形态特征方面的能力，进而改善下游分割任务的性能，具有促进临床工作流程的潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion generative models have demonstrated immense potential for synthetic medical image generation. However, these models often struggle to capture complex morphological characteristics of heterogeneous tumors with irregular boundaries, limiting their utility for downstream clinical tasks such as segmentation. This limitation stems from the standard denoising objective: minimizing a per-pixel error, which smooths high-variance irregular structures characteristic of tumors. To address this, we propose finetuning these generative models with Fréchet Distance loss (FD-loss). FD-loss aligns the first and second order feature statistics of real and generated images in a pretrained encoder space, encouraging the generator to capture complex structural variations characteristic of heterogeneous tumors. We integrate FD-loss across diverse architectural settings, using both natural- and medical-image encoders on multiple liver and brain cancer datasets spanning CT and MRI modalities. Downstream segmentation networks trained on our FD-regularized synthetic data consistently achieve superior performance, improving tumor DSC by $>$$5\%$ over unregularized synthetic augmentation alone. Qualitative analysis suggests these gains are associated with more faithful tumor synthesis and fewer segmentation hallucinations. Our results show FD-loss as an effective regularizer for medical image generative models to improve clinical workflows.

</details>


### [13] [Accuracy Without Grounding: Diagnosing Visual Dependency Dissociation in Video LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13305)
*Jae Joong Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现视频大语言模型在基准测试中的准确率并不一定反映视觉理解能力，提出了视觉依赖差距（VDG）作为衡量标准，发现模型在原始视频和黑屏条件下的表现差异很大，且帧多样性比时间顺序对性能贡献更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频大语言模型的基准测试准确率常被视为视觉理解的证据，但作者质疑这种假设，认为准确率可能来自语言先验而非真正的视觉理解，需要开发方法来评估模型是否真正依赖视觉信息。

Method: 提出了视觉依赖差距（VDG）度量，通过比较模型在原始视频和黑屏条件下的准确率差异来评估视觉依赖程度。设计了诊断梯次实验：从黑屏、单帧、乱序帧到原始视频，还进行了帧率（0.5-24 FPS）消融实验和H.264编码实验。评估了20个模型（2-78B参数，10种架构）和4个API模型。

Result: 1. 模型在原始视频上的准确率差异显著（p=0.0003），但在黑屏条件下无差异（p=0.53）；2. 任务类型排名稳定：属性感知强烈依赖视觉，时间推理接近语言基线；3. 帧多样性提供大部分视觉益处，时间顺序贡献接近零；4. 稀疏采样不是主要原因；5. H.264编码实验显示聚合准确率稳定但掩盖了双向答案翻转；6. API模型VDG范围为0.025-0.315。

Conclusion: 视觉依赖差距（VDG）应作为视频基准测试的标准审计工具，以确保测量的确实是视觉基础能力而非语言先验。模型的基准准确率与视觉理解能力是分离的，需要谨慎解释视频LLM的评估结果。

Abstract: Benchmark accuracy in video large language models (LLMs) is often treated as evidence of visual understanding. We audit this assumption across twenty models spanning 2-78B parameters and ten architecture families. We introduce the Visual Dependency Gap (VDG), the difference in per-question correctness between original-video and black-screen conditions. Paired McNemar tests on MVBench show that accuracy and visual dependency are separable: models differ on original video (p = 0.0003) but not on black screens (p = 0.53). Across models, task-type rankings are stable: Attribute Perception is strongly visual, whereas Temporal Reasoning approaches the language-only baseline. A diagnostic ladder from black screen to single frame, shuffled frames, and original video reveals that frame diversity supplies most of the visual benefit, while temporal order contributes near-zero accuracy across sixteen open-weight models. An ablation from 0.5 to 24 FPS rules out sparse sampling as the cause. H.264 experiments further show that stable aggregate accuracy conceals bidirectional question-level answer flips. The diagnostic also generalizes to four API-accessed models, whose VDG values range from 0.025 to 0.315. These results motivate VDG as a standard audit for whether video benchmarks measure visually grounded capability. Code is available at https://github.com/JaeLee18/accuracy-without-grounding.

</details>


### [14] [Reflecting Process Expertise in Procedural Material Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13318)
*Kunal Gupta,Gaurav Joshi,Yen-Ru Chen,Seemandhar Jain,Ishit Mehta,Manmohan Chandraker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于过程推理的程序化材质生成方法，将专家工作流程表示为文本过程轨迹，利用LLM合成与编译，从教程视频提取知识，生成更易编辑且符合专业策略的材质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化材质创建在数字内容创作、视觉特效和3D资产设计中至关重要，但高质量结果不仅需要复制节点图，还需理解专家构建材质的过程。现有方法多关注静态图合成，缺乏对专家工作流程的建模，导致生成结果不易编辑且与专业设计策略脱节。

Method: 研究将程序化材质生成重新定义为基于专家演示的检索时过程推理，提出过程轨迹作为工作流程的文本表示（包含构建步骤、参数和设计意图）。利用预训练的LLM-based ProcessSynthesizer根据用户意图合成过程轨迹，再通过LLM-based Compiler将其编译为可执行的Blender材质图。从教程视频中提取过程知识，使用自动化视频分析工具生成LLM兼容的文本轨迹。

Result: 在专家研究中，五位Blender艺术家（平均7.5年经验）评估发现，基于专家演示生成的材质工作流程需要更少的编辑，且更贴近专业设计策略。在150名参与者用户研究中，该方法在生成和编辑性能上优于现有程序化系统。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的基于过程推理的程序化材质生成方法，通过将专家工作流程表示为过程轨迹，并利用LLM进行合成与编译，能够生成更符合专业设计策略且易于编辑的材质，显著提升了程序化材质生成的效率与质量。

Abstract: Procedural material creation underpins applications in digital content creation, visual effects, and 3D asset design. Achieving high-quality results requires more than reproducing node graphs -- it demands understanding the process by which experts construct materials. We formulate procedural material generation as retrieval-time process reasoning over expert demonstrations, elevating process to a first-class representation beyond graph-only synthesis. Concretely, we represent expert workflows as process traces: textual records of construction steps, parameters, and design intent. To instantiate this idea, we use a pretrained LLM-based ProcessSynthesizer to synthesize a process trace aligned with a user's intent and a pretrained LLM-based Compiler to ground the process trace into an executable Blender material graph. Because procedural expertise is most naturally conveyed through demonstrations, we leverage tutorial videos as a source of process knowledge and extract textual, LLM-compatible traces using automated video analysis tools. In an expert study with five Blender artists (avg. 7.5 years of experience), materials generated by reflecting expert demonstrations were found to produce workflows requiring fewer edits, and more closely match professional design strategies than methods operating solely on static artifacts. A user study with 150 participants further shows that our approach achieves superior generation and editing performance compared to prior procedural systems. All code, models, and data will be available at https://materialapprentice.github.io

</details>


### [15] [SARFA: Segment Anything with Radiomic Feature Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13323)
*Tyler Ward,Abdullah Imran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SARFA（Segment Anything with Radiomic Feature Alignment）是一个改进医学图像分割的新框架，通过概率提示生成多样化的掩膜候选，并使用基于Fréchet Radiomic Distance和Direct Preference Optimization的放射组学驱动训练目标进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM模型在分割任务中表现出较强的泛化能力，但在目标模糊的情况下（如医学图像中的肿瘤分割）表现不佳。医学图像中专家对目标边界也存在分歧，因此需要解决模糊分割问题并提高分割准确性。

Method: 1. 使用概率提示为每个输入图像生成一组多样化的合理掩膜候选；2. 通过Fréchet Radiomic Distance（FRD）最小化预测掩膜区域与真实标注区域之间的放射组学特征距离；3. 采用Direct Preference Optimization（DPO）对候选掩膜进行排序优化；4. 训练目标关注解剖和纹理特征的临床一致性，而非仅仅像素级重叠。

Result: 在计算机断层扫描（CT）和磁共振成像（MRI）基准测试中，SARFA优于现有的模糊分割方法，证明了放射组学特征对齐和DPO风格候选掩膜排序作为训练目标的有效性。

Conclusion: SARFA通过整合放射组学特征对齐和偏好优化，有效解决了医学图像中模糊目标的准确分割问题，为临床诊断提供了更可靠的分割结果。

Abstract: The Segment Anything Model (SAM) has demonstrated strong generalizability across a variety of segmentation tasks. However, SAM often struggles in situations where the target to be segmented is ambiguous. This poses a problem in medical imaging, where accurate delineation of targets such as tumors is vital, but even expert radiologists can disagree on the appropriate boundary for a target. Addressing this, we propose SARFA (Segment Anything with Radiomic Feature Alignment), a novel framework for improved medical image segmentation. Via probabilistic prompting, SARFA generates a diverse set of plausible masks for each input image and optimizes them with a radiomics-driven training objective based on Fréchet Radiomic Distance (FRD) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). By minimizing the FRD between masked predicted and ground truth regions within each image, SARFA encourages segmentation outputs whose anatomical and textural characteristics align with clinically meaningful ground truth representations, without relying solely on pixel-level overlap. Evaluated on computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) benchmarks, SARFA outperforms existing ambiguous segmentation methods, demonstrating the effectiveness of radiomic feature alignment and DPO-style candidate mask ranking as a training objective. Our code is available at https://github.com/tbwa233/SARFA.

</details>


### [16] [Delving into the Temporal Challenges of Unified Video Protection Against Image-to-Video and Fine-Tuning-based Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13336)
*Yuxin Huang,Ziming Hong,Mingming Gong,Wanyu Wang,Jing Zhang,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TC-UAP是一种针对视频定制的对抗性保护方法，通过优化时间一致的身份级多帧通用对抗扰动，有效抵御基于参考和基于调优的视频定制攻击，并具有时间一致性和对未知视频的保护能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着扩散基视频生成模型的发展，高质量个性化视频定制技术（包括基于调优和基于参考的方法）带来了严重的隐私、身份所有权和知识产权保护问题。现有的反定制研究主要关注图像保护，而针对视频在参考基和调优基定制双重威胁下的保护研究仍很缺乏，面临三个主要挑战：图像级扰动无法抵抗3D视频VAE的时间压缩；视频级扰动对时间编辑脆弱且无法保护未见视频；时间不一致的扰动对时间攻击不鲁棒。

Method: 提出TC-UAP方法：1）通过滑动窗口在多个视频上优化身份级多帧通用对抗扰动，考虑视频VAE中时间压缩引起的局部时间依赖性；2）引入内在时间建模和外在代理时间攻击损失，确保扰动的时间一致性；3）采用跨多个视频的训练策略，使单个扰动能保护各种长度的未见视频。

Result: 实验结果表明，TC-UAP在参考基和调优基视频定制场景下均实现了最强的身份保护效果，相比现有方法有显著优势。该方法对多种未见时间攻击保持鲁棒性，定量和定性评估均验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: TC-UAP是首个针对参考基和调优基视频定制的保护方法，有效解决了视频保护中的三个关键挑战：时间压缩鲁棒性、对未见视频的泛化能力和时间一致性，为视频隐私和安全保护提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Recent diffusion-based video generation models have enabled high-quality personalized video customization through both tuning-based pipelines, which fine-tune a video diffusion model, and reference-based pipelines such as image-to-video generation. However, these capabilities raise serious concerns about personal privacy, identity ownership and intellectual property protection. Existing anti-customization works focus on protecting images, while protection for videos against both reference- and tuning-based customization remains largely underexplored. Protecting videos in this setting raises three challenges: (i) Image-level perturbations, optimized frame by frame, cannot survive temporal compression by 3D video VAE. (ii) A video-level perturbation optimized on a single video is vulnerable to temporal editing and fails to protect unseen videos. (iii) Temporally inconsistent perturbations are not robust to temporal attacks. To address these challenges, we propose Temporally Consistent Universal Adversarial Perturbations (TC-UAP), the first protection method against both reference- and tuning-based video customization. TC-UAP optimizes an identity-level multi-frame UAP over sliding windows from multiple videos, accounting for local temporal dependencies induced by temporal compression in video VAE and enabling a single perturbation to protect unseen videos of varying lengths. Moreover, we introduce intrinsic temporal modeling and an extrinsic surrogate temporal-attack loss, which make the perturbation temporally consistent and robust to unseen temporal attacks. Empirically, quantitative and qualitative results show that TC-UAP achieves the strongest identity protection compared with existing methods under both reference- and tuning-based video customization, and remains robust under multiple unseen temporal attacks.

</details>


### [17] [Marker-free deformable registration and fusion for augmented reality-guided positive margin localization during tumor resection surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13343)
*Yue Yang,Annie Benson,Matthieu Chabanas,Jason Slagle,Thomas Myles,Matthew B. Weinger,Jon S. Heiselman,Michael I. Miga,Michael Topf,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需标记物的增强现实工作流，用于将头颈肿瘤标本的边缘定位映射到患者切除床，显著提高了阳性边缘定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头颈肿瘤手术中阳性边缘的定位存在挑战：病理学家在切除、变形的标本切片上识别阳性边缘，而外科医生只能通过口头描述在切除床上重新定位相应部位，缺乏视觉引导。

Method: 开发了无标记物增强现实工作流，结合轮廓约束变形、深度扫描残差对齐、基于表面的头戴显示器融合以及目标点投影到重建的切除床。使用珠子-缝线对应关系估计标本变形，患者到显示器的融合无需外部基准标记。

Result: 变形目标误差：脸颊7.63±3.74 mm，头皮3.72±1.02 mm；标本到床残差距离：脸颊2.43±2.15 mm，头皮2.19±1.06 mm。无标记融合误差2.15±0.87 mm。端到端边缘定位误差：口头指导21.40±3.84 mm，标本检查16.09±4.30 mm，AR指导6.19±1.79 mm (p<0.001)。在线融合时间5.23±0.34秒。

Conclusion: 该方法有效实现了无标记AR引导下的阳性边缘定位，支持更精确的肿瘤切除，展示了AR技术在手术导航中的实用价值。

Abstract: Positive margins in head and neck oncologic surgery require mapping specimen-side pathology findings to the patient resection bed. This is challenging because pathologists identify the positive margin on slices of the resected, deformed specimen, while surgeons must relocate the corresponding site on the resection bed using only verbal descriptions and no visual guidance. We present a marker-free augmented reality (AR) workflow for mapping a margin label from a three-dimensional specimen scan to the resection bed. The method combines contour-constrained deformation, residual alignment to a depth scan, surface-based fusion to a head-mounted display, and target projection onto the reconstructed bed. Bead-suture correspondences estimate specimen deformation, whereas patient-to-display fusion does not require external fiducial markers. Following formative experiments, five residents and surgeons performed cadaveric cheek and scalp re-resection tasks under verbal guidance, verbal guidance with specimen examination, and AR guidance. Deformation target errors were $7.63 \pm 3.74$ mm for the cheek and $3.72 \pm 1.02$ mm for the scalp; residual specimen-to-bed distances were $2.43 \pm 2.15$ mm and $2.19 \pm 1.06$ mm, respectively. Fusion error did not differ significantly between marker-free and marker-based methods on either cadaver; overall marker-free fusion error was $2.15 \pm 0.87$ mm. End-to-end margin localization error decreased from $21.40 \pm 3.84$ mm with verbal guidance and $16.09 \pm 4.30$ mm with specimen examination to $6.19 \pm 1.79$ mm with AR guidance ($p < 0.001$). Online fusion required $5.23 \pm 0.34$ s. These results demonstrate effective marker-free AR guidance for positive-margin localization and support more precise tumor resection.

</details>


### [18] [Audio-Text Cross-Attention with Psycholinguistic Support Features for Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13345)
*Luiz F. B. F. Martins,Rodrigo W. Pisaia,Matheus M. Girardi,Isabella Berkembrock,João A. Almeida,André G. Hochuli,Rayson Laroca,Alceu S. Britto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于情感矛盾/犹豫视频识别挑战的音频-文本系统，仅使用音频和文本特征而不依赖视觉帧，通过时间交叉注意力融合和门控多实例学习池化，在公共开发集上达到了0.875的平均精度和0.72的宏观F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对第11届ABAW竞赛中的Ambivalence/Hesitancy（情感矛盾/犹豫）视频识别挑战，需要开发有效的多模态方法来识别视频中的犹豫和矛盾情感表达。传统方法通常包含视觉特征，但本文探索仅使用音频和文本模态的可行性。

Method: 1. 将视频分割为重叠的5秒窗口，并与转录时间戳对齐
2. 提取三种特征：320维韵律音频描述符、768维情感导向RoBERTa嵌入、74维人工构建特征（捕捉不确定性、规避和态度冲突）
3. 通过时间交叉注意力融合音频和文本特征
4. 在门控多实例学习池化前注入支持特征以调节窗口重要性
5. 使用五个独立初始化的模型集成，通过平均预测获得最终结果

Result: 在标记的公共开发集上，集成模型达到了0.875的平均精度和0.72的宏观F1分数。该结果表明仅使用音频和文本特征（排除视觉帧）在情感矛盾/犹豫识别任务上具有良好性能。

Conclusion: 提出的音频-文本系统在不使用视觉信息的情况下，通过有效的特征提取、多模态融合和多实例学习机制，在情感矛盾/犹豫识别任务上取得了竞争性的性能。该方法为多模态情感分析提供了新的视角，证明了音频和文本特征的充分性，代码已开源。

Abstract: We present an audio-text system for the Ambivalence/Hesitancy Video Recognition Challenge of the 11th ABAW Competition. The method excludes visual frames and represents each video as overlapping 5-second windows aligned with transcript timestamps. Each window combines a 320-dimensional prosodic audio descriptor, a 768-dimensional emotion-oriented RoBERTa embedding, and 74 handcrafted features capturing uncertainty, hedging, and attitudinal conflict. Audio and text are fused via temporal cross-attention, while support features are injected prior to gated multiple-instance learning (MIL) pooling to modulate the window's importance. Predictions from five independently initialized models are averaged. On the labeled public development set, the ensemble achieved an average precision of 0.875 and a macro-F1 of 0.72. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Liga-de-IA-PUCPR/abaw-11-ah-challenge/.

</details>


### [19] [Detector Confidence Signals Presence Rather Than Occlusion in Cluttered Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13361)
*Yuanzhi He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 开放词汇目标检测器的置信度对遮挡不敏感，即使目标几乎完全被遮挡，检测器仍以高置信度报告目标存在，这表明置信度反映的是类别存在而非目标可见性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测器的置信度被广泛解读为可见性信号，用于阈值化检测、评估开放词汇检测器、语言接地、实例检索和主动感知的门控。然而，当目标被遮挡时，检测器置信度变化极小，甚至可能上升，这与真实可见性严重不符，这在实际应用中可能产生误导。

Method: 通过将每个视图与几何分割oracle配对，获得无检测器的真实可见性地面真值。使用三种检测器(Grounding DINO、OWLv2、SAM3)，在九个对象类别、两个具有不同渲染器和对象集的模拟器、构建和自然遮挡以及真实视频上进行系统审计。评估检测置信度与真实可见性的相关性。

Result: 当真实可见性从完整可见下降到仅八分之一可见时，检测置信度几乎保持不变且与可见性无相关性。检测器在大约90%的场景中报告目标存在，即使目标被严重遮挡，主要是对同类别干扰物做出响应。置信度反映的是类别存在性，而非特定目标可见性。

Conclusion: 基于置信度的指标严重低估了解遮挡的价值(在主动感知设置中8分对88分)，且置信度门控机制恰恰在目标被遮挡时触发。单个视图信号无法标记遮挡问题，因为遮挡物位于目标位置。建议使用目标接地的信号进行门控和评估。

Abstract: Occlude a named object until about an eighth of it remains visible, and an open-vocabulary detector's confidence that the object is present barely changes; as the clutter around it grows the confidence can even rise. On real video the detector still reports the object present in 99% of occluded frames, on another instance of the same category. This matters because that confidence is widely read as a visibility signal, used to threshold detections, evaluate open-vocabulary detectors, ground language, retrieve instances, and gate active perception. We audit whether it reflects occlusion by pairing every view with a geometry-segmentation oracle that gives detector-free ground-truth visibility. As true visibility falls from every scene to one in eight, the confidence stays nearly constant and uncorrelated with visibility, and the detector reports the target present in about nine of ten scenes, firing on same-category distractors: it signals that the category is present somewhere, not that the specific target is visible. The failure holds across three detectors (Grounding DINO, OWLv2, and Segment Anything Model 3), nine object categories, two simulators with different renderers and object sets, built and natural occlusion, and real video. Two consequences follow: a confidence-based metric understates the value of resolving occlusion by about ten times (8 against 88 points in our active-perception setting), and a confidence-based gate fires exactly when the object is hidden. No single-view signal we tried, including a realizable localization check, flags the occlusion, because the occluders sit where the target is. We connect the effect to detector miscalibration and object hallucination, release the controlled benchmark, and recommend target-grounded signals for gating and evaluation.

</details>


### [20] [DiffGI: Differentiable Geometry Images for High-Fidelity Thin-Shell 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13365)
*Eungjune Shim,Hansol Lee,Eunjung Ju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiffGI 是一个端到端的 3D-to-2D 映射框架，通过连续的 TSDF 表示和可微分的 Marching Squares 算法，解决了现有几何图像方法在表示薄壳和非流形几何结构时的分辨率依赖性和信息丢失问题，实现了高保真度的 3D 表面重建和生成，且计算效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 3D 生成模型主要依赖隐式体积表示，这些表示强制要求水密拓扑结构，难以表示服装等薄壳和非流形几何结构。基于几何图像的方法提供了一种以表面为中心的替代方案，但现有方法依赖离散的二元占用图，其分辨率相关的边界编码会导致阶梯状伪影和下采样时的信息丢失，且表面重建仍然是一个与学习流程脱节的非可微分后处理步骤。

Method: 提出了 Differentiable Geometry Image (DiffGI) 框架，将 3D 表面映射到连续的 2D 截断符号距离函数 (TSDF) 中，以亚像素精度编码边界位置。在此基础上，开发了基于解析线性插值的可微分 Marching Squares 算法，使 3D 表面损失的梯度能够反向传播到 2D 潜在空间。利用该可微分流程，训练了一个增强了几何感知法线渲染损失的 DiffGI-VAE，将复杂的 3D 表面压缩到超紧凑的 32x32 潜在空间，并在此空间上实例化了一个基于 Transformer 的潜在扩散模型，用于条件 3D 生成。

Result: 在服装和物体数据集上进行的大量实验表明，与先前的几何图像和基于体素的方法相比，该方法在重建保真度和边界精度方面表现更优，同时所需的计算资源显著减少。

Conclusion: DiffGI 框架通过引入连续的 TSDF 表示和可微分的 Marching Squares 算法，成功解决了现有基于几何图像的 3D 生成方法在表示薄壳和非流形几何结构时的分辨率依赖性和信息丢失问题。该方法在保持高重建保真度和边界精度的同时，显著降低了计算资源需求，为复杂 3D 表面的高效生成和压缩提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing 3D generative models predominantly rely on implicit volumetric representations, which enforce watertight topology and struggle to represent thin-shell and non-manifold geometries such as garments. Geometry image-based approaches offer a surface-centric alternative, but existing methods rely on discrete binary occupancy maps whose resolution-dependent boundary encoding causes staircase artifacts and information loss upon downsampling, while surface reconstruction remains a non-differentiable post-processing step disconnected from the learning pipeline. To address this, we propose Differentiable Geometry Image (DiffGI), an end-to-end 3D-to-2D mapping framework that seamlessly integrates surface representation and geometric optimization. DiffGI replaces binary maps with a continuous 2D Truncated Signed Distance Function (TSDF), which encodes boundary position at subpixel precision within a fixed grid resolution, eliminating resolution-dependent staircase artifacts even under aggressive downsampling. Building on this continuous field, we introduce a differentiable Marching Squares algorithm based on analytical linear interpolation, allowing gradients from 3D surface losses to propagate back to the 2D latent space. Leveraging this differentiable pipeline, we train a DiffGI-VAE augmented with a geometry-aware normal rendering loss to compress complex 3D surfaces into an ultra-compact 32X32 latent space, and instantiate a transformer-based latent diffusion model with a flow-matching objective on top of this space for conditional 3D generation. Extensive experiments on garment and object datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior reconstruction fidelity and boundary precision compared to prior geometry-image and voxel-based approaches, while requiring significantly fewer computational resources.

</details>


### [21] [RoughNet: Mapping Arctic Sea Ice Roughness Using Diffusion-Based Super-Resolution of Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13371)
*Tessa Cannon,Michel Tsamados,Petru Manescu,Thomas Newman,Christian Haas,Veit Helm,Weibin Chen,Randall Scharien*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoughNet：一个基于条件扩散模型的框架，能够从Sentinel-2光学卫星影像直接重建高分辨率海冰粗糙度，提供可扩展的海冰地形测绘方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确估算固定海冰粗糙度对气候建模和北极冰上安全旅行至关重要。现有方法依赖昂贵的航空测量或稀疏的现场测量，空间覆盖范围有限，操作可扩展性不足。

Method: 提出RoughNet方法，采用条件扩散框架，学习将10米分辨率的Sentinel-2多光谱影像映射到局部归一化的1米表面高程残差场。在北极两个区域的航空激光雷达数据上进行训练，在第三个未见区域进行评估。

Result: 最佳模型在域外数据上实现9厘米的均方根误差，同时保留了底层粗糙度场的统计和光谱特性。模型能够泛化到不同的冰况，并部分再现小尺度地形结构。

Conclusion: 生成扩散模型能够仅从光学影像恢复具有物理意义的地表结构，为数据稀疏环境中的高分辨率海冰测绘和粗糙度估算提供了可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of landfast sea ice roughness is critical for climate modeling and safe Arctic over-ice travel, yet existing approaches rely on costly airborne surveys or sparse in-situ measurements, limiting spatial coverage and operational scalability. Here we show that high-resolution sea ice topography can be reconstructed directly from optical satellite imagery using a conditional diffusion framework. Our approach, RoughNet, learns to map 10 m Sentinel-2 multispectral images to locally normalized 1 m surface elevation residual fields, enabling fine-scale roughness characterization from widely available satellite data. Trained on airborne LiDAR data from two Arctic regions and evaluated on an unseen third Arctic region, the model generalizes across diverse ice conditions and partially reproduces small-scale topographic structure. The best-performing model achieves an out-of-domain root mean squared error of 9 cm while preserving the statistical and spectral properties of the underlying roughness field. These results demonstrate that generative diffusion models can recover physically meaningful surface structure from optical imagery alone, providing a scalable pathway for high-resolution sea ice mapping and roughness estimation in data-sparse environments.

</details>


### [22] [FM$^2$: Unified Federated Foundation Models for Heterogeneous Multimodal Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13386)
*Shengchao Chen,Ting Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FM²是一个用于医学影像的联邦基础模型框架，通过在重叠和非重叠模态场景下使用双专家混合模块和异构模态对齐正则化器，有效解决了医学影像领域的数据隐私和模态异质性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像领域需要构建基础模型，但隐私法规禁止集中数据聚合。现有联邦基础模型要么在自然图像模型上微调导致医学领域迁移效果差，要么在单一模态内从头训练缺乏统一任务的能力。特别需要解决未充分探索的挑战——成像模态异质性，包括重叠模态（共享模态但标签分布异质）和非重叠模态（客户端间完全不相交模态）两种结构体系。

Method: 提出了FM²统一框架：1）从头训练核心骨干网络以保持医学领域保真度，可选整合生物医学预训练编码器进行视觉-语言对齐；2）为每个客户端配备双专家混合模块（类级MoE用于个性化类别知识，域级MoE用于共享跨模态表示）；3）异构模态对齐正则化器，显式对齐模态特定专家参数，保证可证明的O(1/√T)收敛和泛化保证；4）引入字幕增强学习，使用本地保留的GPT-4o生成字幕作为文本语义桥梁，实现跨不相交模态客户端的表示迁移，并可扩展到联邦医学视觉问答。

Result: 在提出的MIMH基准测试（分类和字幕增强学习）和真实世界医学视觉问答数据集上的实验表明，FM²在所有三个任务上都一致优于最先进的联邦基线，并展现出强大的跨模态泛化能力。

Conclusion: FM²框架通过双专家混合模块和异构模态对齐策略，成功解决了医学影像联邦学习中模态异质性的核心挑战，同时保持数据隐私，为构建统一的医学影像基础模型提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Building foundation models for medical imaging requires pooling data across institutions, yet privacy regulations prohibit centralized aggregation. Existing Federated Foundation Models either fine-tune natural-image models with poor medical-domain transfer, or train from scratch within a single modality, lacking the flexibility to unify tasks. We identify an under-explored challenge, Imaging Modality Heterogeneity, where clients operate under two structural regimes: Overlapped (shared modalities with heterogeneous label distributions) and Non-overlapped (fully disjoint modalities per client). We propose FM$^2$, a unified framework that trains the core backbone from scratch to preserve medical domain fidelity while optionally incorporating biomedical pretrained encoders for vision-language alignment. FM$^2$ equips each client with dual Mixture-of-Experts modules (a Class-wise MoE for personalized category knowledge and a Domain-wise MoE for shared cross-modality representations), coupled with a Heterogeneous Modality Alignment (HMA) regularizer that explicitly aligns modality-specific expert parameters, admitting provable $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ convergence and generalization guarantees. FM$^2$ further incorporates Caption-Enhanced Learning (CEL), where locally retained GPT-4o-generated captions serve as a textual semantic bridge enabling representation transfer across clients with disjoint modalities, and demonstrates extensibility to Federated Medical VQA. Experiments on our MIMH benchmark (classification and CEL) and real-world medical VQA datasets confirm consistent superiority over state-of-the-art federated baselines and strong out-of-modality generalization across all three tasks.

</details>


### [23] [AnomExpert: Identifying and Selecting Anatomical Planes for Prenatal Ultrasound Anomaly Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13409)
*Jian Wang,Yang Yang,Ziheng Pan,Xiliang Zhu,Yuhan Zhang,Yanfeng Zhou,Dong Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnomExpert是一种仅使用病例级别监督的原型驱动框架，用于产前超声异常诊断，通过可学习的平面原型组织无序图像，并结合疾病感知稀疏选择机制选取诊断相关平面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生命限制性先天异常需要准确的产前诊断进行临床决策。现有自动化方法要么依赖平面级别标注，要么在未显式建模诊断能力的情况下聚合异质图像，无法有效处理产前超声多平面诊断的需求。

Method: 提出了AnomExpert框架：1）引入可学习的平面原型，在不需要平面标注的情况下将无序图像组织成对应解剖平面的潜在表示；2）疾病感知稀疏选择机制，为每个异常选择诊断相关的平面；3）基于Vision Transformer-small骨干网络实现。

Result: 在多中心3,654个病例数据集上的实验表明，AnomExpert在9种代表性多示例学习方法中表现一致最优。使用ViT-small骨干网络时达到86.9%准确率和84.2% F1分数，同时保持参数效率。

Conclusion: 建模解剖平面识别和疾病特异性平面选择能够改进弱监督的多平面产前超声异常分类方法。该方法在仅使用病例级别监督的情况下实现了高质量的诊断性能。

Abstract: Life-limiting congenital anomalies require accurate prenatal diagnosis for appropriate clinical decision-making. Prenatal ultrasound (US) examinations involve multiple anatomical planes, and diagnosis depends on identifying anatomical planes and selecting diagnostically relevant planes for each anomaly. Existing automated methods either rely on plane-level annotations or aggregate heterogeneous images without explicitly modeling these diagnostic capabilities. We propose AnomExpert, a prototype-driven framework for prenatal US anomaly diagnosis using only case-level supervision. AnomExpert introduces learnable plane prototypes to organize unordered images into latent representations corresponding to anatomical planes without requiring plane annotations. A disease-aware sparse selection mechanism further selects diagnostically relevant planes for each anomaly. Experiments on a multi-center dataset of 3,654 cases show that AnomExpert consistently outperforms nine representative multi-instance learning methods. Using a ViT-small backbone, it achieves 86.9% accuracy and 84.2% F1-score while maintaining parameter efficiency. These findings indicate that modeling anatomical plane identification and disease-specific plane selection improves weakly supervised multi-plane prenatal US anomaly classification. The code is available at https://github.com/TIanCat/AnomExpert.

</details>


### [24] [MultiAnimate: A Unified Framework for Controllable Multi-Character Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13415)
*Zhongyi Zhang,Guangyuan Wang,Li Hu,Wenbo Zhou,Peng Zhang,Tianyi Wei,Weiming Zhang,Bang Zhang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MultiAnimate：一个能够同时动画多个角色并保持身份一致性和空间关系的综合框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要专注于单张参考图像的字符动画，这在多角色交互动画等场景中存在局限性。需要解决多角色同时动画的问题，同时保持身份一致性和正确的空间关系。

Method: 1. 身份特定参考网络：从多个参考图像提取外观特征；2. 身份感知姿态编码器：通过注意力机制区分和处理多个姿态序列；3. 交互指导模块：利用角色特定掩码信息处理复杂角色间交互。

Result: 广泛的实验和消融分析证明该框架在多角色动画中的优越性，特别是在涉及复杂运动序列的场景中。

Conclusion: MultiAnimate成功填补了多角色动画的空白，通过创新机制实现了多角色同时动画，同时保持了身份一致性和空间关系，为复杂交互场景提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative models and technological innovations have significantly addressed the fundamental challenges of character image animation. However, existing approaches predominantly focus on character animation from a single reference image, substantially limiting their applicability in scenarios such as multiple character interaction animation. To fill this gap, this paper introduces MultiAnimate, a comprehensive framework that enables concurrent animation of multiple characters within a shared environment while preserving both identity consistency and spatial relationships. The framework achieves these objectives through multiple well-designed mechanisms. First, we incorporate an identity-specific reference net that enables appearance extraction from multiple reference images, distinguishing MultiAnimate from existing approaches constrained to single reference inputs. Second, we implement an identity-aware pose encoder to address the character-pose binding challenge, wherein an attention mechanism enables the network to accurately differentiate and process multiple pose sequences during generation. Third, we introduce an interaction guider module that enhances the framework's capability to handle complex inter-character interactions by leveraging character-specific mask information, serving as an optional component that refines the pose sequences. Extensive experiments and ablation analyses demonstrate our framework's superiority in multiple character animation, particularly in scenarios involving complex motion sequences.

</details>


### [25] [ScanFocus: A Coarse-to-Fine Framework for Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13421)
*Kai Chen,Ming Dai,Wenxuan Cheng,Wankou Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScanFocus是一个用于时空视频定位(STVG)的粗到细框架，通过解耦全局时空扫描和局部边界聚焦来解决现有方法在全局上下文建模与精确边界定位之间的平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的STVG方法在处理长视频时面临计算成本过高的问题，通常采用低速率时间下采样和隐式运动建模，这抑制了高频边界线索并忽略了精确边界划分所需的显式帧间依赖关系。

Method: 1) 使用统一的视觉语言融合编码器结合轻量级可变形语义-运动融合模块进行多模态特征对齐并生成粗略建议；2) 在细化阶段引入语义引导时间聚合器(SGTA)，通过在粗略边界周围密集采样，在语义指导下显式建模短期时间交互以捕捉快速运动变化。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的基准测试上进行了大量实验，证明该方法相比先前方法具有性能优势。

Conclusion: ScanFrameworks通过解耦全局上下文建模和局部边界细化的方法，有效解决了STVG任务中平衡全局建模与精确定位的挑战，提升了时空视频定位的性能。

Abstract: Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding (STVG) aims to retrieve the visual trajectory of a specific object from a video stream as described by a natural language expression. However, most advanced methods struggle to balance global context modeling with precise boundary localization. Due to the prohibitive computational costs of processing long videos, these approaches typically resort to low-rate temporal downsampling and implicit motion modeling. This inevitably suppresses high-frequency boundary cues and neglects the explicit inter-frame dependencies required for precise boundary delineation. To address these limitations, we present \textbf{ScanFocus}, a novel coarse-to-fine framework that decouples the STVG task into a global spatio-temporal scan and a local boundary focus. Specifically, we utilize a unified vision-language fusion encoder combined with a lightweight Deformable Semantic-Motion Fusion module to efficiently align multimodal features and generate coarse proposals. To recover the suppressed fine-grained details, we introduce the Semantic-Guided Temporal Aggregator (SGTA) in the refinement stage. By densely sampling around coarse boundaries, SGTA explicitly models short-term temporal interactions under semantic guidance, capturing rapid motion changes for precise timestamp regression. Extensive experiments on three widely used benchmarks demonstrate the performance superiority of our proposed method over previous approaches. Code will be released at https://github.com/TenMinutes209/ScanFocus.

</details>


### [26] [CLIP-Guided Label-Free Discriminative Region Scoring for Fine-Grained Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13437)
*Yujie Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个统一的CLIP引导的无标签区域评分框架用于细粒度分类，比较了不同的区域生成方法和评分策略，发现Soft Negative Margin评分表现最佳，随机裁剪在无标签设置下优于SAM分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于CLIP和SAM的方法能够实现免训练的细粒度分类，但尚不清楚不同局部区域和CLIP评分策略如何影响判别性证据的选择，特别是在没有真实标签的情况下。

Method: 提出了一个统一的CLIP引导无标签区域评分框架，评估了基于余弦相似度、边界和熵的评分策略，使用SAM生成的掩码和随机裁剪，并引入了基于全局图像嵌入和局部区域嵌入的两种无标签伪标签变体。

Result: Soft Negative Margin评分表现最强，伪标签评分接近真实标签性能。随机裁剪的伪标签评分在所有数据集上均优于基于SAM的评分，表明随机裁剪在伪标签有噪声时能保留周围信息并提供更稳定的语义上下文。SAM掩码受益于聚合所有区域的嵌入，而随机裁剪在较小的top-k子集上表现更好。

Conclusion: 研究为细粒度分类提供了新的见解，揭示了不同区域生成方法和评分策略在无标签设置下的性能差异，为构建更有效的细粒度分类系统提供了指导。

Abstract: Recent vision models such as CLIP and SAM enable training-free segmentation and semantic encoding for fine-grained classification. A common approach is to compare the representations of segmented image regions with the text prompt embeddings of the corresponding labels. However, it remains unclear how different local regions and CLIP-based scoring strategies affect the selection of discriminative evidence, especially when ground-truth labels are unavailable. In this paper, we propose a unified CLIP-guided label-free region scoring framework for fine-grained classification. The framework evaluates cosine similarity-based, margin-based, and entropy-based scoring strategies using both SAM-generated masks and random crops, and introduces two label-free pseudo-label variants based on global image embeddings and local region embeddings. We conduct experiments on five fine-grained classification datasets to systematically compare different region generation methods and scoring strategies. The results show that Soft Negative Margin scoring achieves the strongest performance, and pseudo-label scoring closely approximates true-label performance. Although SAM produces semantically meaningful masks, random-crop-based pseudo-label scoring consistently outperforms SAM-based scoring across all datasets, suggesting that random crops preserve surrounding information and provide more stable semantic context when pseudo-labels are noisy. In addition, SAM masks benefit from aggregating embeddings from all regions, whereas random crops tend to perform better with a smaller top-k subset. These findings provide new insights for fine-grained classification.

</details>


### [27] [DreamSat-Pose: Spacecraft Pose Estimation from Single-View 3D Reconstructions and Learned 2D-3D Feature Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13449)
*Josiane Uwumukiza,Jocelyn Zhao,Giovanni Lavezzi,Giacomo Battaglia,Paolo Panicucci,Minduli C. Wijayatunga,Victor Rodriguez-Fernandez,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个用于未知航天器单图像形状和姿态估计的新框架，通过冻结的DINOv3 ViT提取图像特征，动态图卷积网络处理重建点云，双流transformer匹配器生成2D-3D对应关系，最终通过PnP求解器恢复6-DoF姿态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对未知目标的6自由度姿态估计任务具有挑战性，因为它需要同时重建目标的形状模型。现有方法在未知航天器形状和姿态联合估计方面存在不足。

Method: 1. 使用冻结的DINOv3视觉transformer提取图像特征
2. 从重建点云使用可训练动态图卷积神经网络编码器计算几何特征
3. 双流transformer匹配器通过交替自注意力和交叉注意力细化描述符，生成软对应关系
4. 将对应关系传递到Perspective-n-Point求解器进行姿态恢复

Result: 在SPE3R数据集上的评估结果显示，使用单幅图像和重建几何形状实现了0.157度的平均指向误差，对未见过的航天器表现出强泛化能力。与当前最先进的FoundationPose基线相比表现可靠。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在单幅图像中同时重建未知航天器的3D形状模型并估计其6自由度相对姿态，通过创新的特征提取和匹配策略实现了高精度姿态估计和良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: 6-DoF pose estimation is a critical task in autonomous rendezvous and proximity operations. In the case of an unknown target, this task becomes challenging as it shall be paired with the reconstruction of the target shape model. In this article, we propose a novel framework for single-shot shape and pose estimation of unknown spacecraft objects. Given a single image, we first reconstruct a 3D shape model of the target, then estimate the relative six-degrees-of-freedom pose by learning dense 2D-3D correspondences. The image features are extracted using a frozen DINOv3 vision transformer, while the geometric features are computed from the reconstructed point cloud using a trainable dynamic graph convolutional neural network encoder. A dual-stream transformer matcher refines descriptors through alternating self- and cross-attention, producing soft correspondences that are passed to a Perspective-$n$-Point solver for pose recovery. We evaluate the method on the SPE3R dataset and consider FoundationPose as a representative baseline for current state-of-the-art capabilities. Results show reliable pose estimates achieving 0.157 degrees mean pointing error using only a single image and reconstructed geometry, demonstrating strong generalization to unseen spacecraft.

</details>


### [28] [Symbiosis-Inspired Knowledge Distillation for Incremental Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13452)
*Mingyue Zeng,De Cheng,Zhipeng Xu,Huaijie Wang,Nannan Wang,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIKD（共生启发知识蒸馏）：一种用于增量目标检测的新方法，通过空间共生蒸馏和语义共生蒸馏两个互补层面，利用目标共生关系来缓解灾难性遗忘，而不是传统的分离式范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增量目标检测方法多采用类别增量学习视角，通过分离特征空间来锐化决策边界。然而，这种分离导向的范式忽略了检测中的目标共生关系，如共同出现和遮挡带来的空间和语义依赖，这些依赖其实能从共享表示中受益。忽视这些依赖会扭曲共享表示，加剧新旧类别混淆，并加速灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出共生启发知识蒸馏（SIKD），在两个互补层面显式利用目标共生关系：1）空间共生蒸馏（SpSD）：聚焦于旧模型对新任务中目标响应高度重叠的共生区域，保留可泛化的旧类别线索，抑制类别特异性偏差和冗余，通过槽对齐监督在匹配空间位置向新模型蒸馏精炼证据；2）语义共生蒸馏（SeSD）：通过为旧类别形成置信度加权原型并对其在旧类别对数上的类间软排名进行对齐，来维持类别层级结构，稳定适应过程中的语义拓扑。

Result: 大量实验证明了该方法的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: SIKD通过显式利用目标共生关系，在增量目标检测中实现了更好的知识保留，缓解了灾难性遗忘问题，优于现有的分离导向方法。

Abstract: Incremental object detection (IOD) aims to extend detectors to new categories while retaining previously acquired knowledge. Existing methods often adopt a class incremental learning perspective, separating feature spaces to sharpen decision boundaries. However, this separation-oriented paradigm may overlook object symbiosis in detection, where co-occurrence and occlusion introduce spatial and semantic dependencies that benefit from shared representations. Ignoring these dependencies distorts the shared representations, exacerbates confusion between old and new classes, and accelerates catastrophic forgetting. To address this, we propose Symbiosis-Inspired Knowledge Distillation (SIKD), which explicitly leverages object symbiosis at two complementary levels. Spatial Symbiosis Distillation (SpSD) focuses on symbiotic regions where the old model responds with high overlap to objects in the new task. It preserves generalizable old class cues, suppresses class-specific bias and redundancy, and distills the refined evidence to the new model at matched spatial locations with slot-aligned supervision. Semantic Symbiosis Distillation (SeSD) maintains class level structure by forming confidence weighted prototypes for old classes and aligning their inter class soft ranks over the old class logits, which stabilizes the semantic topology during adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of the proposed method.

</details>


### [29] [GeoAnchor: Collaborative Reasoning via Latent Decomposition for 3D Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13454)
*Hao Li,Han Fang,Zixin Pan,Xin Wei,Hongbo Sun,Jinglin Xu,Zhiyu Lin,Ye Yuan,Zhongjiang He,Yu Yu,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoAnchor：一个交替文本-潜在推理框架，通过解构3D空间信息为位置、方向和几何三个互补潜在组件，提升了多模态大语言模型在复杂几何场景中的3D空间关系理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs主要依赖符号文本标记来理解3D空间关系，但这种方法无法准确表示连续的几何信息。虽然最近方法使用潜在表示增强推理，但单一潜在类型无法适应多样化的空间任务，导致复杂几何场景中的对齐问题。

Method: 提出GeoAnchor框架，将3D空间信息分解为三个互补组件：位置潜在（对象定位）、方向潜在（关系定向）和几何潜在（场景结构）。这些组件在结构化空间中重组，构建局部证据同时捕获全局上下文。采用从局部空间感知到全面3D理解的协作训练策略。

Result: 在多样且复杂的3D推理任务上进行广泛实验，GeoAnchor表现优于现有最先进方法，验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: GeoAddress通过解构和重组3D空间信息的互补潜在组件，解决了MLLMs在复杂几何推理中的局限性，实现了动态且可解释的3D空间关系理解，为多模态AI系统在三维场景理解方面提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress, understanding 3D spatial relationships from 2D images remains a critical challenge. Existing methods primarily rely on symbolic text tokens, which inherently lack the fidelity to represent continuous geometric information. While recent methods use latent representations to enhance reasoning, relying on a single latent type cannot adapt to the diversity of spatial tasks, leading to misalignment in complex geometric scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose GeoAnchor, an interleaved text-latent reasoning framework. GeoAnchor decomposes 3D spatial information into three complementary components: position latents for object grounding, direction latents for relational orientation, and geometry latents for scene structure. These components are recombined in a structured space to construct local evidence while capturing global context, enabling dynamic and interpretable reasoning. Furthermore, we introduce a collaborative training strategy that guides the model from local spatial perception to comprehensive 3D understanding. Extensive experiments on diverse and complex 3D reasoning tasks demonstrate that GeoAnchor outperforms the state of the art, validating its effectiveness and generalization capabilities.

</details>


### [30] [TreeSRNF: Square-Root Normal Fields for Generative Modelling of the Geometric and Structural Variability in Tree-like 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13456)
*Tahmina Khanam,Hamid Laga,Mohammed Bennamoun,Guanjin Wang,Ferdous Sohel,Farid Boussaid,Anuj Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的数学框架，用于分析和生成具有复杂树状结构的3D对象（如植物和树木），该框架能够同时处理几何形状和分支结构的变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么只考虑树状对象的骨架结构，要么通过分支厚度来近似3D几何形状，无法准确建模树状3D对象的完整几何形态和连接方式。需要一个新的框架来同时精确建模树枝的3D几何形状和它们的连接结构。

Method: 1) 将平方根法向场(SRNF)表示扩展到树状3D对象；2) 构建新的黎曼树形空间，并配备新的黎曼度量来测量表面弯曲、拉伸和结构变化；3) 开发算法计算点对点和分支对分支的对应关系以及复杂3D树之间的测地路径；4) 建立统计分析方法，包括计算均值和变化模式；5) 通过从拟合的概率分布中采样来合成新的树状3D对象。

Result: 提出的框架在真实和合成的植物和树木数据上表现出优异性能，显著超越了现有技术水平。该框架能够有效计算统计摘要（均值和变化模式）并合成新颖的树状3D对象。

Conclusion: 提出的数学框架为树状3D对象的分析和生成提供了一个统一的理论基础，通过黎曼树形空间和相应的度量实现了对几何和结构变形的统一建模，在植物建模和计算机图形学领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce a novel mathematical framework for analyzing and generating complex tree-shaped 3D objects, such as botanical trees and plants, which deform both in their 3D geometry and branching structure. Unlike previous works, which either consider only the skeletal structure of tree-like objects or approximate their 3D geometry using branch thickness, the proposed framework accurately models both the 3D geometry of the tree branches and the way they are interconnected. In this paper, we first generalize the Square Root Normal Fields (SRNF) representation, originally proposed for the statistical analysis of genus-0 surfaces, to tree-shaped 3D objects. We then treat tree-shaped 3D objects as points on a novel Riemannian tree-shape space equipped with a novel Riemannian metric that measures the amount of surface bending and stretching, and structural changes one needs to apply to one 3D tree-shape to align it with another. This way, deformations become trajectories in this novel tree-shape space. We analyze the theoretical properties of this novel tree-shape space and the corresponding metric and develop algorithms for computing point-wise and branch-wise correspondences and geodesic paths between complex 3D trees. We finally show how to use these building blocks for (1) computing statistical summaries, \ie means and modes of variation, of collections of tree-shaped 3D objects, and (2) synthesizing novel tree-shaped 3D objects by sampling from probability distributions fitted to a population of tree-shaped 3D objects. We demonstrate the performance and utility of the proposed framework on real and synthetic plants and botanical trees and show that it significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [31] [2D Rotary Position Embedding for Scene Text Recognition with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13458)
*Zobeir Raisi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出2D-RoPE-STR，一种针对场景文本识别的二维相对位置编码方法，通过各向异性的维度分配和编码器-解码器交叉注意力扩展，显著提升了处理弯曲、旋转和透视变形文本的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer-based场景文本识别方法通常使用一维位置编码，忽视了文本图像的二维空间结构。传统的二维轴向RoPE扩展假设图像大致正方形且各向同性，仅适用于编码器自注意力，而场景文本图像具有明显的各向异性且需要编码器-解码器架构。

Method: 1. 提出各向异性的行/列维度分配策略，根据文本图像的长宽比自适应分配旋转维度；2. 将旋转耦合扩展到编码器-解码器交叉注意力中，使自回归解码步骤能够根据二维布局关注编码器标记；3. 引入诊断协议包括控制消融对比、图像级净胜分歧分析和编码器注意力可视化。

Result: 在六个标准基准测试(IIIT5K、SVT、ICDAR 2013、ICDAR 2015、CUTE80、SVTP)上，改进主要集中在处理弯曲、旋转和透视变形的不规则布局文本场景，通过消融实验验证了各设计选择相对于1D RoPE和2D正弦/可学习编码的优势。

Conclusion: 2D-RoPE-STR通过参数无关的改进有效解决了场景文本识别中的二维空间结构建模问题，特别是在处理偏离水平直线阅读顺序的变形文本布局时表现出显著优势，为编码器-解码器架构的二维相对位置编码提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Scene Text Recognition (STR) remains challenging due to the diversity of text appearances, including curvature, rotation, and perspective distortion. Recent Transformer-based approaches perform well but usually rely on one-dimensional positional encodings that ignore the 2D spatial structure of text images. Axial 2D extensions of Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) exist for vision Transformers, but they assume roughly square, isotropic image content and apply the rotation only within encoder self-attention. Scene text violates both assumptions: crops are markedly anisotropic, and STR models are encoder-decoder, so the decoder must relate its queries to the encoder's 2D layout through cross-attention. We introduce 2D-RoPE-STR, which adapts axial 2D-RoPE to this setting through (1) an anisotropic row/column dimension allocation matched to the aspect ratio of text, and (2) an extension of the rotary coupling into encoder-decoder cross-attention, letting autoregressive decoding steps attend to encoder tokens by their 2D layout, a setting not addressed by prior encoder-only formulations. Both changes are essentially parameter-free and require no architectural redesign beyond the positional-encoding module. We further introduce a diagnostic protocol (a controlled ablation pair isolating only the positional encoding, an image-level net-win disagreement analysis, and encoder attention visualization) that identifies where and why relative 2D position helps: curved, rotated, and perspective-distorted layouts where reading order departs from a straight horizontal line. On six standard benchmarks (IIIT5K, SVT, ICDAR 2013, ICDAR 2015, CUTE80, SVTP), gains concentrate on exactly these irregular layouts, with ablations isolating each design choice against 1D RoPE and 2D sinusoidal and learnable alternatives.

</details>


### [32] [LPM: Industrial-Scale Generative Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13460)
*Bichuan Zhu,Fulin Li,Jiachao Gong,Jinhua Hao,Kai Zhao,Kun Yuan,Pengcheng Xu,Qiang Wang,Qiao Mo,Yanlong Yuan,Yizhen Shao,Yuxiao Hu,Zixi Tuo,Ming Sun,Chao Zhou,Bin Chen,Bin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LPM是一个基于扩散模型的生成式视频修复框架，首个工业级生成式视频修复模型，已部署在快手平台上，显著提升视频质量并减少带宽成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决用户生成内容（UGC）中复杂多样的视频退化问题，需要一个能够处理真实场景视频退化、适用于工业级部署的生成式修复系统。

Method: 采用扩散生成框架，包含大规模数据工程、基础模型训练和高效推理三个核心组件。通过增强的架构设计、渐进式训练策略和时间金字塔推理机制，实现任意长度视频的高保真、时间一致的修复。

Result: 模型已在快手部署，处理视频占约45%的总观看时长，用户体验指标一致提升。在保持相似感知质量下，比特率比快手自研编码器降低20%，年带宽成本节约数亿元。低服务成本使其可集成到Kling等产品中。

Conclusion: LPM证明了生成式视频修复技术可以实现工业级大规模部署，在提升用户体验的同时带来显著的系统级成本效益，具有实际应用价值和可扩展性。

Abstract: We present the Large Processing Model (LPM), a diffusion-based generative framework for photorealistic video restoration under complex, in-the-wild degradations. To our knowledge, LPM is the first generative video restoration model deployed at industrial scale. LPM addresses the diverse degradations in user-generated content (UGC) through a unified system encompassing large-scale data engineering, foundation-model training, and efficient inference. Its enhanced architecture, progressive training strategy, and temporal-pyramid inference mechanism jointly enable high-fidelity, temporally consistent restoration of arbitrarily long videos across the broad content distribution encountered on UGC platforms. LPM has been deployed in production at Kuaishou, where videos processed by the model account for approximately 45% of total viewing time, delivering consistent improvements across key quality-of-experience metrics. Beyond perceptual enhancement, LPM delivers substantial system-level benefits: at comparable perceptual quality, it reduces bitrate by 20% relative to Kuaishou's in-house codec, yielding annual bandwidth cost savings on the order of hundreds of millions. Its low serving cost also enables integration into products such as Kling, demonstrating that generative restoration can be practical, scalable, and cost-effective for large-scale video processing.

</details>


### [33] [HIVE-3D: Hierarchical Voxel Enhancement for High-Quality 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13468)
*Bin Zang,Wenting Zheng,Xiaoliang Luo,Zhiyuan Fang,Shi Li,Lvchun Wang,Wei Yu,Yi Zhao,Tian Xie,Yuchi Huo,Rengan Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HIVE-3D：基于分层体素增强框架的单图像高质量3D场景生成方法，通过粗到细的层次化超分辨率生成高分辨率3D场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法可以从单图像生成令人印象深刻的3D对象，但由于表示分辨率受限，不适合3D场景生成。需要开发能够生成高质量3D场景的方法。

Method: 1. 输入单场景图像生成粗糙初始场景；2. 通过图像分割和基于注意力的检索对齐2D图像组件与3D场景组件；3. 将场景关系组织成分层组件树；4. 提出体素超分辨率模型，为目标实例生成精细化体素，同时保持与粗糙体素的一致性；5. 对每个组件执行从粗到细的分层超分辨率。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法显著优于先前方法，实现了最先进的性能，能够生成高分辨率、高质量的3D场景。

Conclusion: HIVE-3D通过分层体素增强框架成功解决了单图像3D场景生成的挑战，提供了一种有效的粗到细层次化方法，在3D场景生成质量上取得了突破性进展。

Abstract: Recently, a line of works can generate impressive 3D objects from a single image, but they are limited by restricted representation resolution, making them unsuitable for 3D scene generation. In this work, we introduce HIVE-3D, a novel method for high-quality 3D scene generation based on hierarchical voxel enhancement framework. Specifically, given a single scene image as input, we first produce a coarse initial scene, then introduce image segmentation and attention-based retrieval to align 2D image components with 3D scene components. Subsequently, we organize these scene relations into a hierarchical component tree, where nodes closer to the leaves denote finer-grained components. Finally, we propose a voxel super-resolution model that generates refined voxels for the target instance while maintaining strong consistency with the coarse voxels. Equipped with this model, we perform coarse-to-fine hierarchical super-resolution on images and voxels for each component, producing a high-resolution and high-quality 3D scene. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms previous approaches, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [34] [Bring Music The Horizon: Music-Driven 360$^\circ$ Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13471)
*Kai Hsu Tsai,Yong Wei Fu,Hung I Yang,Yu-Chih Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Bring Music The Horizon（BMTH），一种情感感知的音乐驱动360°视频生成管道，通过分析音乐的情感轨迹并转换为视觉引导，生成反映音乐情感动态的沉浸式360°音乐可视化视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐可视化方法存在两个主要问题：一是过度依赖歌词，二是生成类似传统音乐视频的平面、非沉浸式内容。这些方法难以传达音乐的情感动态，也无法提供沉浸式的聆听体验。

Method: 1. 输入歌曲后，首先预测每四小节的效价-唤醒度值，估计情感轨迹；2. 使用EmotiCrafter将情感值转换为情感感知的视觉引导；3. 通过SEGA框架对引导向量进行细粒度语义控制，生成关键帧；4. 应用图像到视频模型生成时间连续的360°视频。

Result: 该方法能够生成反映输入歌曲情感进展和时间结构的360°音乐可视化视频。通过不同流派的歌曲进行展示，并与代表性基线方法From-Sound-To-Sight进行了定性比较。

Conclusion: 提出的BMTH管道通过情感感知方法实现了音乐驱动的360°视频生成，提供了更沉浸式的音乐可视化体验，能够有效传达音乐的情感动态。

Abstract: Music visualization offers a powerful way to enhance listeners' understanding and experience of music by translating auditory signals into visual forms. However, most existing approaches either rely heavily on lyrics or generate flat, non-immersive videos similar to conventional music videos, which limits their ability to convey the emotional dynamics of music and provide an immersive listening experience. We propose Bring Music The Horizon, an emotion-aware pipeline for music-driven 360$^\circ$ video generation. Given an input song, our work first estimates its emotional trajectory by predicting valence-arousal values at the level of every four bars. These values are then converted into emotion-aware visual guidance using EmotiCrafter, and these guidance vectors can be manipulated by the SEGA framework, which provides fine-grained semantic control for keyframe generation. Finally, image-to-video models are applied to the generated keyframes to synthesize temporally continuous 360$^\circ$ videos for immersive music visualization. Our pipeline generates 360$^\circ$ music visualization videos that reflect the emotional progression and temporal structure of the input song. We demonstrate its capability using songs from different genres and provide qualitative comparisons with From-Sound-To-Sight, a representative audio-to-visual generation baseline, on our project page at https://etoile-et-toi-mp3.github.io/BMTH_Project_Page/.

</details>


### [35] [GPOcc++: Unified Sparse Gaussian Occupancy Prediction with Visual Geometry Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13481)
*Changqing Zhou,Yueru Luo,Yulan Guo,Bing Wang,Jie Qin,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV
