TL;DR: GPOcc/GPOcc++ 提出将视觉几何先验转化为占用感知的稀疏高斯表示，用于高效的体积场景建模，并通过统一框架处理多视角和时间序列，在室内外场景中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景理解对于具身智能和自动驾驶至关重要，3D占用提供了一个统一的物体、结构和自由空间表示。然而，从视觉观察中恢复完整的体积表示仍然具有挑战性，特别是在遮挡和未观察区域。视觉几何先验提供强大的几何线索，但其输出本质上是表面中心的，而占用预测需要对体积内部和自由空间进行推理。

Method: 引入GPOcc将视觉几何先验转化为占用感知的稀疏高斯表示，实现高效且富有表达力的体积场景建模。基于GPOcc，GPOcc++在统一框架内建模多视角观察和时间序列，允许通过相同表示处理空间和时间证据。进一步将GPOcc++从室内场景扩展到室外占用预测。

Result: 在室内和室外基准测试上的广泛实验表明，该方法在多视角和时间设置中均表现出持续强大的性能，同时具有良好的效率和泛化能力。

Conclusion: GPOcc/GPOcc++通过将视觉几何先验转化为占用感知的稀疏高斯表示，有效解决了体积场景建模中表面中心先验与体积占用预测之间的差距，为室内外3D场景理解提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate 3D scene understanding is fundamental to embodied intelligence and autonomous driving, where 3D occupancy provides a unified representation of objects, structures, and free space. However, recovering such a complete volumetric representation from visual observations remains challenging, particularly in occluded and unobserved regions. Visual geometry priors offer strong and generalizable geometric cues for addressing this challenge, but their outputs are inherently surface-centric, whereas occupancy prediction requires reasoning about volumetric interiors and free space. To bridge this gap, we introduce GPOcc, which transforms visual geometry priors into occupancy-aware sparse Gaussian representations for efficient and expressive volumetric scene modeling. Building on GPOcc, GPOcc++ models multi-view observations and temporal sequences within a unified framework, allowing spatial and temporal evidence to be handled through the same representation. We further extend GPOcc++ from indoor scenes to outdoor occupancy prediction. Extensive experiments on both indoor and outdoor benchmarks demonstrate consistently strong performance across both multi-view and temporal settings, together with favorable efficiency and generalization. Code will be released at https://github.com/JuIvyy/GPOcc.

</details>


### [36] [CASA-SDF: Curriculum-Aware Spatial Adaptation with Curvature-Guided Density for Neural Implicit Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13492)
*Lei Yang,Weiqing Li,Zhiyong Su,Liang Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CASA-SDF是一个用于3D室内场景重建的统一框架，通过课程感知的空间适应方法，解决平面区域与薄结构之间的几何异质性挑战，实现更好的表面完整性和细节恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内场景重建面临显著的几何异质性挑战：大块无纹理平面区域需要强正则化抑制高频伪影，而薄结构则需要更锐利、自适应的表示来缓解MLP的光谱偏差并防止过平滑。现有方法通常依赖空间无差别的先验监督和场景全局的SDF-to-density变换，限制了平衡平面平滑性和细节保持的能力。

Method: 提出了CASA-SDF框架，包含两个核心组件：1）混合空间自适应不确定性退火（SAUA），融合语义和光度不确定性构建像素级课程，用于单目先验监督；2）曲率感知局部自适应密度变换（CALADT），通过曲率代理逐步调节SDF-to-density映射的锐度以增强薄结构的表示。

Result: 在基准室内数据集上的大量实验表明，CASA-SDF在保持平面表面稳定性的同时，改善了高频结构的表面完整性和细节恢复能力。

Conclusion: CASA-SDF通过监督和表示能力的互补适应，有效解决了室内场景的几何异质性挑战，为高质量室内表面重建提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Neural implicit representations have emerged as a powerful paradigm for 3D reconstruction. However, high-fidelity indoor surface reconstruction remains a significant challenge, primarily due to the pronounced \emph{geometric heterogeneity} of indoor scenes. Large texture-less planar regions typically require stronger regularization to suppress high-frequency artifacts, while thin structures demand sharper, more adaptive representations to mitigate the spectral bias of multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) and prevent over-smoothing. Existing approaches often rely on spatially indiscriminate prior supervision and a scene-global SDF-to-density transformation, which constrains their ability to balance planar smoothness and detail preservation. In this paper, we propose CASA-SDF (Curriculum-Aware Spatial Adaptation for SDF), a unified framework that addresses this challenge via complementary adaptations of supervision and representation capacity. Specifically, Hybrid Spatially-Adaptive Uncertainty Annealing (SAUA) fuses semantic and photometric uncertainties to construct a pixel-wise curriculum for monocular prior supervision. This strategy maintains regularization in reliable regions while attenuating unreliable supervision early in training to enable data-driven photometric refinement. Meanwhile, Curvature-Aware Locally Adaptive Density Transformation (CALADT) progressively modulates the sharpness of the SDF-to-density mapping via a curvature proxy to enhance the representation of thin structures. Extensive experiments on benchmark indoor datasets demonstrate that CASA-SDF improves surface completeness and detail recovery on high-frequency structures, without compromising the stability of planar surfaces.

</details>


### [37] [M2P-AD: Memory-to-Prototype Learning with Boundary-aware Score Refinement for 3D Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13499)
*Seyoung Jeong,Jong Pil Yun,Sang Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出M2P-AD模型，通过原型学习和边界感知分数细化，解决3D异常检测中正常区域过度响应和边界误报问题，在多个数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有方法在3D异常检测中取得了较高性能，但正常区域的过度异常响应和物体边界附近的误报问题仍未解决。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的3D异常检测模型M2P-AD，包含两个核心模块：1) Memory-to-Prototype (M2P)模块，从正常特征嵌入中学习代表性原型，以保留对象的重要结构信息；2) Boundary extraction (BE)模块，用于识别对象边界，并结合边界感知分数细化(BSR)策略，利用边界特征重新校准异常分数。

Result: 在Real3D-AD、Anomaly-ShapeNet和MulSen-AD数据集上评估，取得了最先进的性能。定性结果表明，正常区域的过度异常分数减少，边界附近的误报得到抑制，实现了更准确、更稳定的异常定位。

Conclusion: 该方法在三个基准数据集上实现了最先进的性能，显著减少了正常区域的异常分数和边界附近的误报，从而实现了更准确、更稳定的异常定位，为现实工业环境提供了可靠的3D异常检测方案。

Abstract: 3D anomaly detection has recently emerged as an important research topic in computer vision. Although existing methods have achieved high performance, excessive anomaly responses in normal regions and false positives near object boundaries remain unresolved challenges. To address these challenges, we propose a novel 3D anomaly detection model, Memory-to-Prototype Anomaly Detection (M2P-AD), which effectively models the distribution of normal features while suppressing excessive anomaly scores in normal regions and false positives near object boundaries. Specifically, we introduce a Memory-to-Prototype (M2P) module that learns representative prototypes from normal feature embeddings to preserve important structural information of objects. In addition, a Boundary extraction (BE) module is integrated to identify object boundaries, and a Boundary-aware score refinement (BSR) strategy is applied to recalibrate anomaly scores by incorporating boundary characteristics. The proposed method is evaluated on Real3D-AD, Anomaly-ShapeNet, and MulSen-AD, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Qualitative results demonstrate that excessive anomaly scores in normal regions are reduced and false positives near object boundaries are suppressed, resulting in more accurate and stable anomaly localization. The results indicate that the proposed approach enables more reliable 3D anomaly detection and provides a robust solution applicable to real-world industrial environments.

</details>


### [38] [Attention-Free and Lightweight Token Reduction for Efficient Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13500)
*Xuanyi Hao,Zuoyuan Zhang,Zhibo Wang,Xiaoyi Pang,Jiahui Hu,Jiacheng Du,Shuguo Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需注意力机制且轻量化的token缩减框架，作为即插即用模块用于视觉语言模型，通过信息论熵准则和变换诱导一致性信号来保留重要且多样的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型在多模态理解上表现优异，但在资源受限的边缘设备上部署面临挑战，主要因处理大量视觉token带来的计算开销。现有token缩减方法要么依赖于与现代化加速框架不兼容的注意力图，要么需要计算密集的成对相似性比较，这降低了可扩展性并抵消了实际部署优势。

Method: 提出一个注意力无关的轻量级token缩减框架：1）采用信息论视角，使用基于熵的新准则量化token信息，保留具有更富表达性和较少退化特征表示的token；2）引入变换诱导一致性信号，通过该信号的排序使相似token位置相邻，再通过基于步长的选择获得多样token集合。

Result: 在多个VLM基准测试上的广泛实验表明，该框架实现了良好的准确率-效率权衡，在激进的压缩情况下仍保持竞争性性能。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种高效实用的VLM token缩减解决方案，避免了传统方法的局限性，为边缘设备部署提供了可行的优化途径。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance in multimodal understanding, yet remain challenging to deploy on resource-constrained edge devices due to the substantial computational overhead of processing numerous visual tokens. Token reduction is a promising direction for accelerating VLMs inference, but existing approaches either rely on attention maps that are incompatible with modern acceleration frameworks or depend on computationally intensive pairwise similarity comparisons, which undermine scalability and negate their practical benefits in deployment. In this paper, we propose an attention-free and lightweight token reduction framework as a plug-and-play module for VLMs, which preserves both important and diverse tokens to produce a compact visual representation. First, to enable attention-free importance estimation, we adopt an information-theoretic perspective and quantify token information using a novel entropy-based criterion, retaining those with more expressive and less degenerate feature representations. Second, to ensure diverse visual coverage in a lightweight manner, we introduce a transformation-induced consistency signal where similar tokens yield similar signals, such that sorting by this signal places similar tokens close to each other and enables stride-based selection to produce a diverse token set. Extensive experiments across multiple VLMs benchmarks demonstrate that our framework achieves a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off, maintaining competitive performance under aggressive compression.

</details>


### [39] [DP-BOA: Dirichlet-Process Birth-or-Assign for On-the-Fly Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13504)
*Peiyan Gu,Zixin Teng,Xuming He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了DP-BOA方法，一个基于在线狄利克雷过程高斯混合模型的预测后验决策框架，用于在线的类别发现任务，能够在测试时决定是将新样本分配到现有类别还是创建新类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线类别发现方法通常基于匹配启发式规则（如半径或哈希规则），将新类别生成作为当现有类别匹配不自信时的后备方案，而不是有自身统计证据支持的显式选择。这种隐含处理方式不够理想，需要更统计严谨的决策框架。

Method: 提出DP-BOA框架，基于在线Dirichlet-process高斯混合模型和Normal-Inverse-Wishart先验。训练阶段使用标记数据校准共享的NIW先验并预热已知类别后验。测试时，对每个新样本比较分配到现有类别的后验预测证据与由DP先验诱导的生成新类别证据，然后在线更新类别统计。

Result: 在标准OCD基准测试中，DP-BOA始终优于强基线方法，在维持竞争力的已知类别准确率的同时，实现了特别强的新类别发现性能。方法能够捕获各向异性类别几何形状，并随着证据积累自然调整决策置信度。

Conclusion: DP-BOA为在线类别发现提供了一个统计严谨的决策框架，将新类别生成从隐含后备方案转变为基于证据支持的显式选择，通过后验预测证据比较实现更可靠的类别分配决策。

Abstract: On-the-fly category discovery requires deciding for each incoming test sample whether to assign it to an existing category or spawn a new one. Existing methods typically implement this decision through matching-based heuristics, such as radius- or hash-based rules. While effective in practice, these methods usually treat category birth implicitly as a fallback when no existing category matches confidently, rather than as an explicit alternative supported by its own statistical evidence. To address this, we propose DP-BOA, a posterior-predictive decision framework based on an online Dirichlet-process Gaussian mixture model with a Normal-Inverse-Wishart prior. During training, we use labeled data to calibrate a shared NIW prior over category Gaussians and warm-start the known-category posteriors. At test time, for each incoming sample, DP-BOA compares the posterior predictive evidence for assignment to existing categories against the evidence for spawning a new category induced by the DP prior, and then updates category statistics online after the decision. The method captures anisotropic category geometry and naturally adapts decision confidence as evidence accumulates. Across standard OCD benchmarks, DP-BOA consistently outperforms strong baselines and delivers particularly strong novel-class discovery performance while maintaining competitive known-class accuracy.

</details>


### [40] [TRACE-PCa: Predicting Prostate Cancer Progression from Longitudinal MRI During Active Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13506)
*Hongye Zeng,Shreeram Athreya,Dingyuan Dai,Steve Raman,Leonard Marks,William Speier,Corey Arnold*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需病灶分割的端到端时序多模态模型，用于预测前列腺癌主动监测期间的病理进展，通过时序注意力门机制增强与进展相关的影像变化特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前列腺癌主动监测依赖定期重复活检，但大多数活检结果显示无进展且不必要；现有风险分层工具基于单时间点影像或依赖显式病灶分割，无法捕捉纵向变化且排除无MRI可见病灶的患者。

Method: 1. 使用预训练的3D MRI基础模型编码每次序列扫描；2. 引入时序注意力门机制，重新校准多次就诊特征，放大与进展相关的局部影像变化；3. 将门控影像表征与临床变量在多模态框架中融合，预测进展概率。

Result: 在纵向主动监测队列中验证，该方法持续优于竞争基线，表现与代表当前临床实践的放射科医生评估相当；保持高阴性预测值的同时获得更高阳性预测值。

Conclusion: 该模型有潜力安全减少主动监测期间的不必要活检，为无病灶分割的纵向风险分层提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Active surveillance (AS) is the preferred strategy for favorable-risk prostate cancer, yet current protocols rely on scheduled repeat biopsies, most of which reveal no progression and are unnecessary. Existing risk-stratification tools operate on single time-point imaging or depend on explicit lesion segmentation, limiting their ability to capture longitudinal change and excluding patients without an MRI-visible lesion. In this study, we propose an end-to-end temporal and multimodal model for predicting pathological progression during AS without lesion segmentation. We encode each serial scan with a pretrained 3D MRI foundation model and introduce a temporal attention gate that recalibrates the multi-visit features to amplify focal imaging changes associated with progression. The gated imaging representation is then fused with clinical variables in a multimodal framework to estimate the probability of progression. Validated on a longitudinal AS cohort, our approach consistently outperforms competing baselines and performs comparably to the radiologist assessment representing current clinical practice. It maintains high negative predictive value while achieving higher positive predictive value, demonstrating its potential to safely reduce unnecessary biopsies during surveillance.

</details>


### [41] [Groc-PO: Grounded Context Preference Optimization for Truthful Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13712)
*Zhixiao Zheng,Zheren Fu,Zhiyuan Yao,Chunxiao Liu,Dongming Zhang,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Groc-PO框架，通过构建多阶段偏好数据集并在对象接地、上下文接地和推理阶段引入显式监督，有效缓解了多模态大语言模型的错误传播问题，提升了模型的真实性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型存在视觉幻觉、内容捏造和不忠实推理等不真实性问题，这些问题严重影响了其忠实性和实际应用价值。现有的基于人类偏好的对齐方法（如DPO）通常在最终答案层面进行偏好优化，而多模态推理错误往往在早期接地阶段传播，导致对早期阶段的监督是间接的，难以抑制由接地漂移和上下文不一致引起的错误传播。

Method: 提出了Grounded Context Preference Optimization (Groc-PO)框架，并构建了Grounded Context Preference Dataset (GCPD)，该数据集围绕对象接地、上下文接地和接地推理三个阶段组织多阶段偏好样本，以捕捉接地上下文的形成、整合和利用过程。

Result: 大量实验表明，与标准DPO和其他强基线相比，Groc-PO在幻觉缓解、忠实推理和整体可靠性方面取得了更好的性能，验证了更明确的接地监督对于可信多模态推理的价值。

Conclusion: 研究提出的Groc-PO框架通过引入更明确的多阶段偏好监督，有效增强了多模态大语言模型的上下文依赖推理能力，缓解了跨阶段错误传播，从而提升了模型的可靠性和真实性。

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), they still suffer from untruthfulness issues, such as visual hallucinations, content fabrication, and unfaithful reasoning, which substantially undermine their faithfulness and practical utility. Alignment methods based on human preference, such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), have been widely adopted to address these issues. However, multimodal reasoning errors often propagate across stages, and final-answer errors can often be traced to mistakes in early grounding stages, yet standard DPO typically applies preference optimization at the final-answer level. This credit-assignment challenge means that supervision for early grounding stages is indirect rather than stage-specific, making it difficult to suppress error propagation arising from grounding drift and context inconsistency. To address this, we propose Grounded Context Preference Optimization (Groc-PO), a grounded preference optimization framework for MLLMs. We further construct the Grounded Context Preference Dataset (GCPD), organizing multi-stage preference samples around three stages of Object Grounding, Contextual Grounding, and Grounded Reasoning, to capture the formation, integration, and utilization of grounded context. By introducing more explicit preference supervision over multiple grounded stages, Groc-PO strengthens context-dependent reasoning and mitigates cross-stage error propagation. Extensive experiments show that, compared with standard DPO and other strong baselines, Groc-PO achieves improved performance in hallucination mitigation, faithful reasoning, and overall reliability, supporting the value of more explicit grounded supervision for trustworthy multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [42] [DriveFace: A Cross-Spectral Through-Glass Face Dataset for On-the-Move Vehicular Border Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13515)
*Anjith George,Luis Luevano,Alain Komaty,Zeina Al Amine,Vidit Vidit,Sebastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个用于边境控制场景下的动态面部识别数据集，该数据集包含近红外车辆通过视频与智能手机预注册数据的配对，以解决现有数据集缺乏真实环境挑战的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨境流动性的持续增长给现有边境控制基础设施带来压力，需要开发在车辆运动中进行的生物识别认证技术。虽然面部识别适合这种被动远距离获取的应用场景，但其发展受到缺乏代表性数据集的限制，现有基准数据集在受控环境中收集，未能捕获车辆采集固有的挑战。

Method: 作者引入了一个专为边境控制场景设计的动态面部识别数据集，该数据集包含：1）近红外（NIR）车辆通过视频；2）智能手机预注册数据。这些数据配对收集，以模拟真实边境控制场景。

Result: 使用最先进模型进行的基线评估显示，在这些真实条件下存在明显的性能限制。这表明需要专门的方法来推进该领域的发展。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了动态面部识别研究的关键空白，为开发能够应对车辆采集实际挑战（如运动模糊、光照变化、遮挡和跨光谱注册）的先进算法提供了必要的基础设施。通过揭示现有模型在真实边境控制场景中的局限性，强调了开发专门方法的重要性。

Abstract: The continuous growth in cross-border mobility places increasing pressure on existing border control infrastructures, motivating on-the-move biometric authentication, in which travellers are identified directly inside their vehicles at checkpoints. Face recognition is well-suited to this setting, as it can be acquired passively and at a distance. Its development, however, is hindered by the lack of representative datasets: existing benchmarks are collected in controlled environments and do not capture the challenges inherent to vehicular acquisition, including motion blur, variable illumination, occlusions, and cross-spectral enrollment. To address this gap, we introduce a dataset for on-the-move face recognition in border-control scenarios, comprising NIR vehicle-crossing videos paired with smartphone-based pre-enrollment data. Baseline evaluations with state-of-the-art models show clear performance limitations under these realistic conditions, highlighting the need for dedicated methods to advance the field.

</details>


### [43] [VGIF-Score: Interpretable and Diagnostic Evaluation of Spatio-Temporal Instruction Following in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13527)
*Songyu Xu,Xin Wang,Qiang Chen,Xinran Wang,Muxi Diao,Yuxuan Zhang,Kongming Liang,Rui Lin,Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGIF-Score是一个自动化、可解释的视频生成指令跟随评估框架，通过客观完成度和主观满意度两个分支来全面评估模型对长、结构化提示的遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在视觉保真度方面取得了进展，但对长、组合指令的遵循能力评估不足。当前评估方法通常依赖简短、语义浅显的提示，具有有限的原子约束和弱的时空依赖性，且多依赖昂贵的人工评估或手工视觉管道，缺乏对指令约束成功或失败的诊断性见解。

Method: VGIF-Score包含两个互补组件：1）客观完成分支：将提示解析为时空有向无环图（ST-DAG），并执行依赖感知问答和短路诊断；2）主观满意度分支：使用指令条件化AutoRubric评估摄影技术、视觉纯度、运动平滑度和物理规律遵循。这两个组件共同产生一个统一的分数。

Result: 在VGIF-Bench基准测试上（包含223个长、结构化提示和约4.3K细粒度评估项），对14个专有和开源视频生成模型的3K多个生成视频进行实验，结果显示VGIF-Score能够提供可靠、可解释且具有诊断意义的评估结果。

Conclusion: VGIF-Score是一个有效的评估框架，能够全面评估视频生成模型的指令跟随能力，特别是在处理长、复杂提示时。该框架为研究人员提供了诊断性见解，有助于改进视频生成模型的指令理解能力。

Abstract: Recent video generation models (VGMs) have made substantial progress in visual fidelity, yet their ability to follow long, compositional instructions remains insufficiently evaluated. Existing evaluation protocols often rely on prompts that are short and semantically shallow, with limited atomic constraints and weak spatio-temporal dependencies. They also frequently depend on costly human evaluation or handcrafted vision pipelines, while providing little diagnostic insight into which instruction constraints succeed or fail. To address this gap, we propose VGIF-Score, a highly automated and interpretable framework for evaluating instruction following in video generation. VGIF-Score consists of two complementary components: an objective completion branch that parses prompts into a Spatio-Temporal Directed Acyclic Graph (ST-DAG) and performs dependency-aware QA with short-circuit diagnostics, and a subjective satisfaction branch that uses instruction-conditioned AutoRubric to assess cinematography, visual purity, motion smoothness, and physics adherence. Together, these components produce a unified score that captures both objective completion and perceptual satisfaction. We instantiate this framework on VGIF-Bench, a benchmark of 223 long, structurally entangled prompts paired with approximately 4.3K fine-grained evaluation items. Experiments on 14 proprietary and open-source VGMs across more than 3K generated videos show that VGIF-Score provides reliable, interpretable, and diagnostically useful evaluation of video generation instruction following. The code will be available at https://github.com/PRIS-CV/VGIF-SCORE.

</details>


### [44] [ThinkBLOX: 3D Indoor Scene Generation with Progressive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13539)
*Yuan Xiao,Can Wang,Xiangyu Kong,Jing Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ThinkBLOX：一个基于视觉语言模型的渐进式推理框架，通过迭代设计和细化3D室内场景来解决传统一次性生成方法在交互编辑时的全局优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的一次性3D场景生成方法在交互编辑（如插入或移动物体）时需要全局重新优化或完全重建，且常导致物理或语义上组织不良的排列。现有方法难以处理逐步更新场景的需求。

Method: 1. 提出ThinkBLOX框架，将布局生成视为状态条件化的逐步推理-行动过程；2. 构建ThinkBLOX-Data-200K数据集（224,757个程序化放置对），包含多视角场景上下文、显式思维链原理和结构化JSON布局；3. 通过监督微调使VLM学习增量更新下的推理-行动桥梁；4. 引入Tier-Decoupled GDPO强化学习方案，将异构奖励组织到不同层级，稳定策略优化。

Result: 实验表明ThinkBLOX在物理合理性、语义对齐和交互可编辑性方面显著优于近期的一次性和迭代基线方法。支持多种应用，包括全局和局部的3D场景生成与重排。

Conclusion: ThinkBLOX成功解决了3D场景生成中的逐步推理问题，通过数据驱动的渐进式框架和分层强化学习方案，实现了更灵活、物理合理且语义对齐的场景布局，特别适合交互式编辑应用。

Abstract: While traditional graphics methods often synthesize 3D indoor scenes autoregressively or hierarchically, recent vision-language model (VLM)-based generators predominantly adopt a one-shot paradigm where the full layout is planned at once. This one-shot approach often requires global re-optimization or complete reconstruction during interactive editing (e.g., inserting or moving objects) and can lead to physically or semantically poorly organized arrangements. To address these challenges, we propose ThinkBLOX, a VLM-based progressive reasoning framework that iteratively designs and refines 3D scenes. ThinkBLOX treats layout generation as a state-conditioned, step-by-step reasoningand-action process. To power this, we construct the ThinkBLOX-Data-200K dataset, containing 224,757 procedural placement pairs annotated with multi-view scene context, explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) rationales, and structured JSON layouts. Through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on this dataset, the VLM learns to bridge the reasoning-action gap under incremental updates. Furthermore, recognizing that scene synthesis is inherently a multisolution task where SFT suffers from reward conflict, we introduce Tier-Decoupled GDPO. This reinforcement learning scheme organizes heterogeneous rewards into distinct tiers, stabilizing policy optimization across physical validity, semantic plausibility, and reasoning-action consistency. Extensive experiments show that ThinkBLOX significantly outperforms recent one-shot and iterative baselines in physical plausibility, semantic alignment, and interactive editability. Additionally, we show that it supports diverse applications, including both global and local generation and rearrangement of 3D scenes.

</details>


### [45] [Nexus: Native Mesh Generation with Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13563)
*Hanxiao Wang,Ying-Tian Liu,Yuan-Chen Guo,Qi-Yuan Feng,Zi-Xin Zou,Ding Liang,Biao Zhang,Yan-Pei Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Nexus是一种通过解耦顶点和拓扑生成实现整体网格生成的扩散方法，解决了现有自回归方法在推理效率和错误累积方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成高质量三角形网格对于电影、游戏和交互式3D应用至关重要。主流方法依赖网格序列化和自回归过程，在推理效率方面表现不佳且对错误累积敏感。

Method: 1. 将网格顶点视为八叉树组织的稀疏体素，采用扩散模型以从粗到精的方式生成顶点；2. 提出时空区间作为时空距离的扩展，将任意边和面拓扑编码为连续的每顶点嵌入，实现全局高效的复杂拓扑恢复；3. 使用扩散模型在生成的顶点上生成连续嵌入。

Result: 在Objaverse和Toys4K数据集以及野外图像上的大量实验表明，该方法优于最先进的自回归和两阶段基准方法，有效规避了顺序网格建模的固有局限性。来自3D从业者的盲用户研究证实了对结果的强烈感知偏好。

Conclusion: Nexus通过解耦顶点和拓扑生成，提供了一种高效且稳健的网格生成方法，克服了自回归方法的局限性，在质量和实用性方面表现出色。

Abstract: Generating high-quality triangle meshes is essential for film, gaming, and interactive 3D applications. Mainstream methods rely on mesh serialization and autoregressive processes, which stuggles in effective inference and is sensitive to error accumulation. In this paper, we present Nexus, a diffusion method that achieves holistic mesh generation via decoupled vertex and topology generation. First, we view mesh vertices as sparse voxels organized as an octree and adopt a diffusion model to generate the vertices in a coarse-to-fine manner. Second, for topology modeling, we propose Spacetime Interval, as an extension of Spacetime Distance to encode arbitrary edge and face topology into continuous per-vertex embeddings. It allows for a global and efficient recovery of complex topology. We then employ a diffusion model to generate the continuous embeddings on the generated vertices. Extensive experiments on the Objaverse and Toys4K datasets and in-the-wild images demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art autoregressive and two-stage baselines, effectively circumventing the inherent limitations of sequential mesh modeling. A blind user study from 3D practitioners confirms strong perceptual preference for our results.

</details>


### [46] [GHR-VLM: Making Zero-Shot Transit Video Analytics Realizable with Grounded Hybrid Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13569)
*Kaicong Huang,Weiheng Oh,Ruimin Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GHR-VLM：一个视觉接地的混合推理框架，用于零样本公交视频分析，结合边缘计算追踪和云端VLM推理，实现对乘客上下车和支付行为的精细化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频监控需要任务特定的标注，而直接应用视觉语言模型到长视频流中不可靠且成本高。需要结合两者的优势：通过显式的视觉接地将长监控流转换为紧凑的、以乘客为中心的时空证据，从而提高VLM的推理能力。

Method: 提出边缘-云端混合设计：1) 边缘端轻量级监控器持续追踪车门状态并分割乘客片段；2) 后端VLM通过两阶段粗到细的时空证据细化，识别上下车乘客并分类支付行为；3) 仅在接地的乘客片段和联系单上调用VLM，减少云端推理。

Result: 在486分钟的真实世界公交监控视频上评估，展示了接地边缘-云端推理在乘客级支付分析方面的潜力，同时指出了视频条件退化带来的挑战。

Conclusion: GHR-VLM框架通过视觉接地显著改善了VLM在长视频分析中的性能，减少了云端推理成本，避免了支付特定训练数据的需求，为公交视频分析提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Transit video understanding can provide valuable fine-grained data that conventional passenger counters and fare systems cannot capture. However, supervised video models require task-specific annotations, while applying vision-language models (VLMs) directly to long onboard videos is unreliable and costly. To leverage the complementary strengths of both approaches, we propose GHR-VLM, a visual grounded hybrid reasoning framework for zero-shot transit-bus video analytics. It is motivated by the observation that explicit visual grounding can improve VLM reasoning by converting long surveillance streams into compact, passenger-centered spatiotemporal evidence. Specifically, we propose an edge-cloud design in which a lightweight edge-based monitor continuously tracks door status and segments passenger clips. A backend VLM then identifies boarding passengers and classifies payment behavior through a two-stage coarse-to-fine refinement of spatiotemporal evidence. By invoking the VLM only on grounded passenger clips and contact sheets, GHR-VLM reduces cloud inference, avoids payment-specific training data, and supplies the localized evidence that VLMs otherwise struggle to identify. Evaluation on 486 minutes of real-world bus surveillance video demonstrates the potential of grounded edge-cloud reasoning for passenger-level payment analytics while highlighting the challenges posed by degraded video conditions.

</details>


### [47] [UniPhysGen: Unified Physical Grounding for Simulation-Ready 3D Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13586)
*Xian Li,Rong Wei,Lujie Yang,Haolin Huang,Junyuan Fang,Siliang Tang,Jun Xiao,Rui Tang,Juncheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniPhys框架通过联合推理铰接语义和固有物理属性，将原始3D资产自动转换为具有统一物理语义的仿真就绪资产，并构建了大规模数据集和基准测试


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D资产大多缺乏统一的物理语义（铰接语义和固有物理属性），这限制了在具身AI和机器人仿真中的真实交互。当前方法要么独立处理这些语义，要么依赖于规范化的对象结构，导致在异构3D资产上的鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出UniPhys框架，包含：1) UniPhys-40K：大规模物理基础数据集；2) UniPhys-Bench：统一物理基础评估基准；3) UniPhysGen：联合推理铰接语义和固有物理属性的统一物理基础模型，该模型采用几何鲁棒的铰接基础方法以减少异构部件分解下的几何捷径偏差。

Result: 在铰接基础和固有物理属性估计任务上达到了最先进的性能，生成的资产可以直接部署到机器人仿真环境中实现真实的物理交互。

Conclusion: UniPhys框架成功解决了3D资产缺乏统一物理语义的问题，通过自动化转换和联合推理方法，为具身AI和机器人仿真提供了高质量的物理基础资产，其代码和数据集将开源以促进领域发展。

Abstract: Physically grounded 3D assets are increasingly important for embodied AI and robotic simulation. However, most existing 3D assets lack unified physical semantics, including articulation semantics and intrinsic physical properties, required for realistic interaction. Current approaches either treat these semantics independently or rely on canonicalized object structures, limiting robustness across heterogeneous 3D assets. We present UniPhys, a scalable framework for automatically transforming raw 3D assets into simulation-ready assets with unified physical semantics. Based on UniPhys, we construct UniPhys-40K, a large-scale physically grounded dataset, together with UniPhys-Bench, a carefully verified benchmark for unified physical grounding evaluation. We further introduce UniPhysGen, a unified physical grounding model that jointly reasons over articulation semantics and intrinsic physical properties. UniPhysGen incorporates geometry-robust articulation grounding to mitigate geometric shortcut bias under heterogeneous part decompositions. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across articulation grounding and intrinsic physical property estimation tasks, while the resulting assets can be directly deployed in robotic simulation environments for realistic physical interaction. Our code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/breezexian/UniPhysGen.

</details>


### [48] [OvisOCR2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13639)
*Shiyin Lu,Yinglun Li,Yu Xia,Yuhui Chen,An-Yang Ji,Jun-Peng Jiang,Qing-Guo Chen,Jianshan Zhao,En Lin,Haijun Li,Cheng Qin,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OvisOCR2是一个0.8B参数的端到端文档解析模型，通过数据引擎、强化学习和模型融合等技术，在多个基准测试中实现了最先进的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的文档解析方法主要依赖流水线式处理，存在累积误差和复杂性。作者旨在开发一个端到端的文档解析模型，能够直接从文档图像生成结构化Markdown表示，涵盖文本、公式、表格和视觉区域

Method: 1) 构建数据引擎：结合筛选的真实文档标注和从同一HTML源生成的合成页面；2) 训练方法包括有监督微调、使用多组件奖励设计的强化学习（在4B分支上）、策略蒸馏到0.8B模型、模型融合

Result: 在OmniDocBench v1.6上获得96.58分的最高总评分，在PureDocBench上获得75.06的Avg3最高分，在内部基准测试中也表现最佳，显示了其泛化能力和鲁棒性

Conclusion: OvisOCR2展示了端到端文档解析模型的潜力，超越了传统流水线方法，在多个基准测试中实现了最先进的性能，为文档理解任务提供了有效的解决方案

Abstract: We introduce OvisOCR2, a 0.8B document parsing model. OvisOCR2 is designed as an end-to-end parser: given a document page image, it generates a Markdown representation in natural reading order, covering text, formulas, tables, and visual regions. We build a data engine that combines filtered real-document annotations with synthetic pages whose rendered images and Markdown targets are derived from the same HTML source. The training recipe includes supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning on a 4B branch with a multi-component reward design, on-policy distillation into the 0.8B model, and model fusion. On OmniDocBench v1.6, OvisOCR2 achieves a state-of-the-art overall score of 96.58, placing an end-to-end model at the top of this leaderboard previously dominated by pipeline methods and highlighting the potential of end-to-end document parsing. On PureDocBench, OvisOCR2 also achieves the highest Avg3 score of 75.06. Beyond these two public benchmarks, we evaluate OvisOCR2 on an in-house benchmark designed to cover a broader set of long-tail and challenging scenarios. OvisOCR2 obtains the best overall performance among the compared methods, providing further evidence of its generalization and robustness. OvisOCR2 is available at https://huggingface.co/ATH-MaaS/OvisOCR2.

</details>


### [49] [Human4K: A Large-Scale 4K Multi-View Mocap Dataset for Whole-Body 3D Human Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13646)
*Tianshun Han,Ziyu Shi,Lijian Liu,Ajian Liu,Benjia Zhou,Hugo Jair Escalante,Yanyan Liang,Sergio Escalera,Zhen Lei,Jun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Human4K是一个大规模4K多视角全身人体重建数据集，包含超过600万4K图像和运动捕捉精确的SMPL-X标注，旨在解决现有数据集在图像分辨率、标注精度和运动多样性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前3D人体重建方法在最具挑战性的现实场景中仍然失败，产生不稳定的几何形状、不准确的肢体关节和在深度模糊或自遮挡下的不可靠预测。主要原因是现有数据集缺乏高分辨率图像、高精度标注和多样化全身运动的组合来支持稳健的重建。

Method: 提出Human4K数据集，包含超过600万4K图像，通过八视角高分辨率相机系统采集，并与专业Vicon运动捕捉系统同步。涵盖11名执行复杂、高度关节化和强烈自遮挡全身动作的受试者。所有序列通过运动重定向和精炼模块(MRRM)处理，确保全身和肢体的精确对齐。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用Human4K进行训练能持续改进标准基准上的全身重建性能，特别在手部、脚部和深度模糊的肢体配置方面有显著提升。

Conclusion: Human4K数据集填补了现有数据在高质量图像、精确标注和复杂运动多样性方面的空白，为更稳健的3D人体重建提供了重要资源，特别是在处理具有挑战性的深度模糊和自遮挡情况时表现出色。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D human reconstruction have improved overall performance, yet current models still fail in the most challenging real-world scenarios. They often produce unstable geometry, inaccurate limb articulation and unreliable predictions under depth ambiguity or self-occlusion. A key reason is that existing datasets still lack the combination of high-resolution images, high-precision annotations and diverse whole-body motions required to support robust reconstruction. To address this gap, we present Human4K, a large-scale 4K multi-view whole-body human reconstruction dataset with mocap-accurate SMPL-X annotations. Human4K contains over six million 4K images captured by an eight-view high-resolution camera system synchronized with a professional Vicon motion capture setup, covering 11 subjects performing complex, highly articulated and strongly self-occluded full-body motions. All sequences are processed by a Motion-Retargeting and Refinement Module (MRRM) to ensure precise alignment for the full body and extremities. Experimental results show that training with Human4K consistently improves whole-body reconstruction on standard benchmarks, with particularly large gains for hands, feet and depth-ambiguous limb configurations.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Color Geometry: Evaluating Human-Like Color Representations in Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13647)
*Ayan Igali,Pakizar Shamoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文评估了视觉模型是否以类似人类的方式感知颜色，提出了基于模糊感知模型的评估框架，发现MAE模型在超越几何对齐方面表现最佳


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有颜色表示评估通常使用几何空间（如CIELAB）或离散颜色标签作为参考，这些方法只捕捉感知距离或类别归属，无法反映人类对颜色的分级组织方式。需要评估视觉模型与人类颜色感知的契合程度。

Method: 提出基于模糊感知模型的评估框架，该模型包含86个分级类别，拟合了人类调查数据。框架可应用于任何图像编码器，测量三个互补属性：类别边界、类别紧凑性以及超越颜色几何的解释能力的分级对齐。

Result: 在11个Vision Transformer编码器中，类别级结果相似，但分级对齐差异显著。Masked Autoencoders（MAE）在超越几何对齐方面表现最强，其置信区间与其他编码器不重叠。层分析显示掩码重建在输出方向保留了此结构。在自然图像上，MAE全局表示表面颜色，而语言监督模型更多将颜色编码与前景对象相关联。

Conclusion: 人类般的颜色感知具有多个不同方面，不应简化为单一评分。MAE模型通过掩码重建机制更好地保留了人类颜色组织的分级结构，提供了超越传统几何颜色空间的评估视角。

Abstract: Do vision models see colors the way humans do? Existing evaluations of color representations usually compare them with geometric spaces such as CIELAB or with discrete color labels. These references capture perceptual distance or category membership, but not the graded way in which people organize colors. We evaluate color grounding against a fuzzy perceptual model with 86 graded categories fitted to human survey data. The framework can be applied to any image encoder and measures three complementary properties: category boundaries, category compactness, and graded alignment beyond what color geometry alone can explain. Across eleven Vision Transformer encoders, the category-level results are broadly similar, whereas graded alignment differs substantially. Masked Autoencoders achieve the strongest beyond-geometry alignment, with confidence intervals that do not overlap those of the other encoders. A layer-wise analysis further shows that masked reconstruction preserves this structure toward the output. On natural images, MAE represents surface color globally, while language-supervised models encode color more strongly in relation to the foreground object. These results show that human-like color grounding has several distinct aspects that should not be reduced to a single score.

</details>


### [51] [From Surface Forecasting to Observability Forecasting: A Latent World Model for Cloud-Aware EO Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13651)
*Mohanad Albughdadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究将LeWorldModel适配到地球观测序列中，用于预测下一次采集的可用性及恢复时间，在多个基准测试中显著优于持久性模型，并在连续回归和精确时间预测上优于LightGBM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测处理链的瓶颈不在于新图像的到达，而在于图像到达时地表是否实际可见。因此，研究可观测性预测问题，以预测下一次采集是否可用，以及不可用时何时可能恢复可用视图。

Method: 将LeWorldModel（联合嵌入预测架构世界模型）适配到云感知的地球观测序列中，构建包含5个图像通道（蓝、绿、红、近红外、云掩膜）和8个气象与日历协变量的HDF5序列数据集，训练一个拥有1800万可训练参数的模型。

Result: 在完整的冻结捆绑可观测性基准测试中，LeWorldModel持续优于持久性模型。对于下一步可用性，平衡准确率在0.769到0.887之间（持久性为0.493到0.556）；对于首次可用时间精确预测，准确率在0.602到0.806之间（持久性为0.120到0.369）。与LightGBM基线相比，LeWorldModel在连续晴/云回归和精确恢复时间预测上表现更好，但在简单的六步内任何可用性二元任务上LightGBM更强，且在OOD上更稳健。

Conclusion: LeWorldModel在可观测性预测任务中表现出色，特别是在连续晴/云回归和精确恢复时间预测方面优于LightGBM基准，但在某些简单任务和OOD稳健性上仍有提升空间。

Abstract: The bottleneck of Earth Observation processing chains is not the arrival of new imagery but whether the surface is actually visible when the image arrives. We study this as an observability forecasting problem on EarthNet2021. Given recent multispectral imagery and exogenous weather drivers, the goal is to predict whether the next acquisition will be usable and, if not, when a usable view is likely to return. To do this, we adapt LeWorldModel, a joint-embedding predictive architecture world model, to cloud-aware Earth Observation sequences. The final pipeline converts raw minicubes into episodic HDF5 sequences with five image channels (blue, green, red, near-infrared, cloud mask) and eight meteorological and calendar covariates. The resulting model has 18.0M trainable parameters and is trained from scratch on 23,904 training episodes. The trained leWorldModel is evaluated under a locked protocol: linear probes are fit on train only, calibration choices are set on an internal validation split, and the fitted heads are then frozen for valsplit, IID, OOD, and extreme evaluation. On the full frozen-bundle observability benchmark, LeWorldModel consistently outperforms persistence. For next-step usability, balanced accuracy ranges from 0.769 to 0.887, compared with 0.493 to 0.556 for persistence. For exact first-usable-horizon prediction, accuracy ranges from 0.602 to 0.806, compared with 0.120 to 0.369 for persistence. Against a frozen LightGBM baseline fit on the same training windows, LeWorldModel is better on continuous clear/cloud regression and on exact recovery timing on valsplit, IID, and extreme, while LightGBM is stronger on the simpler binary any-usable-within-six task and is more robust on OOD. In separate sampled diagnostic analyses, LeWM also produces strong ranking-based anomaly signals under synthetic temporal inconsistencies.

</details>


### [52] [Exploratory, Communicative, and Deployable: Vision-Driven Embodied Agents for Open-World Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13653)
*Boyu Mi,Mengchen Ma,Yifei Yao,Xing Gao,Junting Chen,Yangzi Li,Zihou Zhu,Guohao Li,Zhenfei Yin,Tai Wang,Yao Mu,Jiangmiao Pang,Hanqing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REAL是一个用于开放世界移动操作的智能体框架，通过模拟一致的API和用户模拟支持人类在环交互，在交互任务上表现优于商业VLMs（56.9%成功率），并在真实机器人上实现78.3%的端到端成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架通常依赖特权模拟器状态或假设完整指令，忽略了真实世界部署中面临的主动探索、视觉接地和交互意图消歧等挑战。需要弥合现实部署差距。

Method: 提出REAL框架：1) 建立无需特权感知的模拟一致的API；2) 集成模拟用户支持人类在环交互；3) 设计多样化任务组合驱动数据收集、监督微调和在线强化学习；4) 引入REAL-Bench基准（241个任务），覆盖主动探索、视觉干扰、关节操作和交互消歧。

Result: 1) 在交互任务上训练代理超越领先商业闭源VLMs，达到56.9%成功率；2) 层次训练流程成功对齐模型工具使用能力，同时保持扩展探索视野下的稳健开放词汇推理；3) 在真实双臂移动机器人上部署，60个真实世界情景中达到78.3%端到端成功率，展示了未见家庭场景的零样本可迁移性。

Conclusion: REAL框架通过模拟一致的设计成功弥合了长视野移动操作的现实差距，验证了其在真实世界部署中的有效性。框架已开源，为开放世界移动操作提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world deployment of embodied agents requires active exploration, visual grounding, and interactive intent disambiguation. However, existing frameworks often rely on privileged simulator states or assume complete instructions, bypassing realistic deployment challenges. To bridge this gap, we present REAL, an agentic framework for open-world mobile manipulation. REAL establishes sim-to-real-consistent environment APIs without oracle perception and integrates a simulated user to enable human-in-the-loop interaction. Within this environment, we design diverse task compositions to drive data collection, supervised fine-tuning, and online reinforcement learning, systematically optimizing agent performance. To comprehensively evaluate this approach, we introduce REAL-Bench, a benchmark spanning 241 tasks across active exploration, visual distraction, articulated manipulation, and interactive disambiguation.
  Experimental results demonstrate that our trained agent outperforms leading commercial closed-source VLMs on interactive tasks with a 56.9% success rate. Further empirical analysis reveals that our hierarchical training pipeline successfully aligns the model's tool-use capabilities while maintaining robust open-vocabulary reasoning under extended exploration horizons. Finally, we deploy and evaluate our framework on a physical dual-arm mobile robot, where it achieves a 78.3% end-to-end success rate over 60 real-world episodes. These physical trials demonstrate robust zero-shot transferability to unseen household scenarios, validating that our sim-to-real-consistent design successfully bridges the reality gap for long-horizon mobile manipulation. Code is available at https://github.com/InternRobotics/REAL.

</details>


### [53] [T3HG-Editor: Text-driven 3D Human Garment Editing with Body Priors Embedded in SMPL-X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13654)
*Shaoru Sun,Xingtao Wang,Zihan Ma,Wenrui Li,Jiantao Zhou,Debin Zhao,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T3HG-Editor：基于SMPL-X先验的文本驱动3D人体服装编辑器，通过三阶段流程实现高保真和服装一致的编辑效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D高斯编辑方法主要将2D编辑技术应用于多视角渲染图像，然后基于修改后的图像更新3D高斯。将这种方法扩展到3D人体服装编辑会导致低保真结果，存在扭曲和服装不一致的问题。SMPL-X模型提供了丰富的虚拟人体先验信息，为解决这一问题提供了突破机会。

Method: T3HG-Editor包含三个阶段：1)可编辑高斯获取：沿SMPL-X法线播种高斯生成足够的近表面高斯，再通过2D掩码约束精确定位待编辑目标高斯；2)服装一致编辑：聚合多视角中对应同一SMPL-X顶点的token并传播回原始视角，无需额外训练即可强制执行服装一致性；3)带溢出剪枝的高斯更新：使用SMPL-X上定义的SDF构建人体距离场，结合2D语义掩码剪枝溢出高斯，防止非目标区域污染。

Result: 在多个主体和多样服装类型上的实验表明，T3HG-Editor在编辑质量和服装一致性方面均优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: T3HG-Editor通过有效利用SMPL-X的几何和关节先验，成功解决了文本驱动3D人体服装编辑中的低保真和服装不一致问题，实现了高质量的编辑效果。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Editing (3DGE) has seen substantial progress, text-driven 3D human garment editing remains largely underexplored. Existing 3DGE works typically follow a paradigm that applies 2D editing techniques to multi-view rendered images and updates 3D Gaussians based on the modified images. Extending such methods to 3D human garment editing suffers from low-fidelity outcomes, caused by introduced distortions and garment inconsistencies. A promising breakthrough opportunity arises from the SMPL eXpressive (SMPL-X) model that embodies rich prior information for virtual humans. Motivated by this insight, we propose a text-driven 3D human garment editor termed T3HG-Editor, which delivers high-fidelity and garment consistent results by leveraging geometry and joint priors embedded in SMPL-X. Specifically, T3HG-Editor contains three stages, namely obtainment of editable Gaussians, garment consistent editing, and Gaussian updating with overflow pruning. The obtainment of editable Gaussians begins with seeding Gaussians along SMPL-X normals to generate sufficient near surface Gaussians, followed by a 2D mask constraint that precisely localizes the target Gaussians to be edited. The garment consistent editing aggregates tokens corresponding to the same SMPL-X vertex across multiple views and propagates them to their original views, enforcing garment consistency without requiring additional training. Gaussian updating with overflow pruning employs a Signed Distance Function (SDF) defined on SMPL-X to construct a human distance field, which is then integrated with a 2D semantic mask to prune overflowing Gaussians, thus preventing contamination of non-target regions. Experiments on multiple subjects and diverse garment types demonstrate that T3HG-Editor outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both editing quality and garment consistency.

</details>


### [54] [Barnamala: Parameter-Efficient Handwritten Devanagari Recognition at Benchmark Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13689)
*Ashish Thapa,Samrat Karki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一个仅1.11M参数的紧凑卷积网络，在46类Devanagari字符识别任务上达到99.73%准确率，比之前SOTA模型小15.6倍，且发现了11个错误的内在性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前Devanagari手写字符识别系统参数过多，需要开发更紧凑高效的模型。同时发现该数据集存在性能饱和现象，所有模型都达到相同的内在错误下限。

Method: 设计并训练了一个紧凑的卷积神经网络（1.11M参数），用于46类Devanagari字符识别。进行了广泛的实验，包括与大型模型基线和教师集成模型的比较，使用McNemar检验和Wilson置信区间进行统计显著性分析。

Result: 在DHCD数据集上达到99.73%准确率，超越先前SOTA且模型尺寸小15.6倍。发现11个错误的内在性能上限（误差约0.27%）。在CMATERdb数字数据集上，零样本准确率76.6%，微调后达97.8%。抗噪性显著优于大型基线模型（平均抗噪准确率75.7% vs 38.7%）。

Conclusion: 紧凑网络在保持或超越性能的同时大幅减少参数，证明模型大小并非决定性能的关键因素。DHCD数据集存在内在性能上限，所有模型都收敛于相同的错误率。小模型在抗噪性和泛化能力方面表现更优。

Abstract: We built a compact convolutional network (1.11 M parameters) for 46-class DHCD Devanagari recognition and reached 99.73%, the highest reported at 15.6x smaller than prior state-of-the-art. We have effectively reached the saturation point: every model tested, large teacher ensembles included, hits the same 11-error intrinsic floor. No configuration achieves a statistically clear win under exact McNemar tests with Wilson confidence intervals. Even without knowledge distillation, our student matches the nearest large-model baseline (17.32 M parameters; McNemar $p = 0.345$). Outside of DHCD, zero-shot on CMATERdb digits gives 76.6% and fine-tuning reaches 97.8%; corruption robustness is also far better than large baselines (mean corruption accuracy 75.7% vs. 38.7%). All artifacts are at https://github.com/Ampixa/barnamala.

</details>


### [55] [FreeLit: Paired-Free Indoor Relighting via Physics-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13656)
*Chi-En Yen,Duy-Khanh Ngo,Wen-Wei Tang,Huu-Phu Do,Wen-Hsiao Peng,Ching-Chun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeLit：无需配对监督的室内场景重光照框架，通过物理引导的光照先验和内在稳定化策略实现可控的光源位置、颜色和强度调节


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图像的室内场景重光照面临复杂几何与局部光照交互的挑战。现有数据驱动方法依赖昂贵的多光照配对数据集，而逆向渲染方法虽然减少数据依赖但缺乏在挑战性条件下的内在估计鲁棒性

Method: 1. 构建物理引导的光照先验：从内在场景属性生成结构化光照图及伪重光照图像指导扩散合成
2. 重光照引导的内在稳定化策略：通过结构感知蒸馏和一致性约束强制光照不变反射率，解决低光照场景中的内在估计不稳定问题
3. 引入可控性导向的评估指标量化与用户指定光照颜色和强度的对齐度

Result: 实验结果表明，FreeLit实现了稳定、物理一致且可控的重光照效果，在低光照室内场景中表现出改进的鲁棒性，且无需配对监督

Conclusion: FreeLit通过结合物理先验和扩散合成，解决了配对数据稀缺和内在估计不稳定问题，为可控室内重光照提供了有效解决方案，实现了在没有配对监督情况下的稳定和精确光照控制

Abstract: Image-based indoor scene relighting remains challenging due to the complex interplay between cluttered geometry and local illumination, requiring precise modeling of light position, color, and intensity. Existing data-driven methods implicitly learn this relationship via paired multi-illumination datasets. Nevertheless, this data is costly and fails to scale, which is essential for accurate light-source-level control. Conversely, inverse-rendering methods reduce the data dependency by incorporating physical priors; however, they lack the robustness of intrinsic estimation in challenging conditions.
  In this paper, we present FreeLit, a paired-free framework for controllable indoor relighting that explicitly manipulates light-source location, color, and intensity. Instead of relying on paired supervision, we construct a physics-guided illumination prior from intrinsic scene properties, generating a structured lightmap along with a pseudo-relit image to guide diffusion-based synthesis. To address instability in intrinsic estimation, especially in low-light scenes, we introduce a relighting-guided intrinsic stabilization strategy that enforces illumination-invariant reflectance through structure-aware distillation and consistency constraints. Furthermore, we propose controllability-oriented evaluation metrics to quantify alignment with user-specified illumination color and intensity. Experimental results demonstrate that FreeLit achieves stable, physically consistent, and controllable relighting, with improved robustness in low-light indoor scenes, without requiring paired supervision.

</details>


### [56] [Fine-grained CLIP fine-tuning with self-annotated region alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13661)
*Chenyang Zhao,Wei Lin,Antoni B. Chan,Janet H. Hsiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SFF-CLIP方法，通过自标注的细粒度微调方案，仅使用图像-文本对即可增强CLIP的细粒度稠密特征表示能力，同时保持其原有的全局视觉语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP在预训练时侧重于将整个图像与文本描述匹配，导致其细粒度稠密特征表示能力有限。现有增强CLIP细粒度能力的方法存在以下问题：1) 需要额外的区域标注，这由于预定义类别而限制语义多样性，且数据处理工作量大；2) 通常会牺牲CLIP原有的全局视觉表示能力。

Method: 提出SFF-CLIP（Self-annotated Fine-grained Fine-tuning for CLIP），仅使用图像-文本对作为输入。核心设计是运行时区域-短语对齐方案：1) 从输入句子中提取概念短语；2) 使用文本特定的热图将提取的区域特征与对应概念短语对齐。

Result: 大量实验表明，SFF-CLIP在细粒度稠密特征表示方面带来显著的性能提升，同时在图像级任务上保持了原始CLIP的性能。

Conclusion: SFF-CLIP是一种有效的自标注细粒度微调方法，仅需图像-文本对即可增强CLIP的细粒度表示能力，同时维持其原有的全局视觉语义一致性，避免了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) has been shown to have limitations in its fine-grained dense feature representation, due to its pre-training focusing on matching the whole image to a text description. Considering the large data and computational burden in pre-training a vision-language model from scratch, a series of works aim to enhance the fine-grained ability of CLIP through a fine-tuning scheme. However, existing works suffer from a variety of limitations: additional region annotations are usually required, which limits the semantic diversity due to the predefined categories and leads to a large effort to process the training data; and they usually sacrifice CLIP's original ability for global visual representation. To bypass these limitations, we propose SFF-CLIP (Self-annotated Fine-grained Fine-tuning for CLIP), which only uses image-text pairs as input to boost the fine-grained representation ability in the CLIP fine-tuning, while maintaining the global visual-semantic consistency. Concretely, a run-time region-phrase alignment scheme is designed, which obtains concept phrases from the input sentence, and aligns them with corresponding extracted region-based features using text-specific heat maps. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SFF-CLIP leads to significant performance improvements on fine-grained dense feature representation, as well as maintaining the performance of the original CLIP on image-level tasks. Code will be released later.

</details>


### [57] [Anatomically Faithful but Temporally Blind: Auditing Attribution for Left-Ventricular Ejection-Fraction Estimation from Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13738)
*Hyunkyung Han,Min Jung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于深度学习的心超视频EF评估模型虽然空间定位准确，但在时间维度上无法识别关键帧（收缩末期和舒张末期），即使可解释性方法显示空间可信度，也无法保证时间层面的决策可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前使用可解释性方法（如Chefer Relevance和Grad-CAM）验证深度学习模型"关注了正确区域"的做法日益普遍，但这些解释在空间和时间上的可信度均未被审计。由于EF的定义依赖于收缩末期（ES）和舒张末期（ED）关键帧，一个可信的解释必须在空间上定位左心室，在时间上定位决定性帧。

Method: 研究在EchoNet-Dynamic数据集上微调了两个EF回归模型：自监督的VideoMAE Transformer和Kinetics预训练的R(2+1)D CNN。使用架构匹配的可解释性方法对每个模型从三个维度进行审计：1）与左心室掩模的交并相关比（IoR），2）删除曲线下面积（AUC），3）针对ES/ED关键帧的时间定位指数。每个指标都相对于随机基线，并在50个研究中计算每案例95%置信区间。同时使用tubelet遮挡探针来区分可解释性失败与模型行为。

Result: 两个模型在空间维度均具有可信度：VideoMAE的IoR比随机基线高2.91倍，R(2+1)D高1.98倍。但在时间维度上，两个模型的时间定位指数与随机基线无显著差异（0.97-1.00），甚至不优于随机可解释性。遮挡实验显示模型并未优先依赖ES/ED帧（仅为随机基线的0.90倍），表明时间盲性是模型行为本身，而非可解释性方法缺陷。

Conclusion: 空间可信度并不保证时间可信度。可解释性方法可以认证模型的解剖学定位准确性，但同时可能掩盖模型忽略临床关键帧的事实。这对基于XAI验证视频诊断模型提出了警示，并呼吁需要引入时间感知的训练和评估方法。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Deep video models estimate left-ventricular ejection fraction (EF) from echocardiography with near-expert accuracy, and post-hoc attribution (Chefer relevance for transformers, Grad-CAM for CNNs) is increasingly used to certify that models "look at the right place." Yet whether these explanations are faithful both spatially and temporally is unaudited. Because EF is defined by the end-systolic (ES) and end-diastolic (ED) frames, a faithful explanation must localize the left ventricle (space) and the decisive frames (time).
  Methods: We fine-tune two distinct EF regressors on EchoNet-Dynamic -- a self-supervised VideoMAE transformer and a Kinetics-pretrained R(2+1)D CNN -- and audit each with architecture-matched attribution along three axes: intersection-over-relevance (IoR) against LV masks, deletion AUC, and a temporal localization index on ES/ED frames, each relative to chance with per-case 95% CIs over 50 studies. A tubelet-occlusion probe separates attribution failure from model behavior.
  Results: Both models are anatomically faithful -- IoR 2.91x (VideoMAE) and 1.98x (R(2+1)D) above chance -- yet temporally blind: temporal localization is indistinguishable from chance (0.97--1.00) and no better than random attribution. Occlusion shows the models do not preferentially rely on ES/ED (0.90x chance), so temporal blindness reflects model behavior, not an attribution artifact.
  Conclusions: Spatial faithfulness does not imply temporal faithfulness. Attribution can certify anatomical grounding while masking that a model ignores the clinically decisive frames -- a caution for XAI-based validation of video diagnostic models and a call for temporally-aware training and evaluation.

</details>


### [58] [Learning Speaker Identity Beyond Language and Modality Constraints: Insights from the POLY-SIM 2026 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13669)
*Marta Moscati,Muhammad Saad Saeed,Marina Zanoni,Mubashir Noman,Rohan Kumar Das,Monorama Swain,Yassin Terraf,Yufang Hou,Elisabeth Andre,Khalid Mahmood Malik,Markus Schedl,Shah Nawaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: POLY-SIM 2026挑战赛旨在解决多模态说话人识别系统中面临的现实挑战：模态不完整性（音频或视觉信息缺失）和多语言说话人识别（跨语言变异性）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的多模态说话人识别系统面临两个主要问题：1）模态不完整性——由于遮挡、设备故障或隐私约束导致音频或视觉信息缺失；2）多语言说话人——现有系统通常假设每个说话人只说单一语言，但现实中说话人常使用多种语言，语言变异性增加了识别难度。这些假设在现实应用中往往不成立，对系统的鲁棒性和泛化能力构成挑战。

Method: 通过POLY-SIM 2026挑战赛提供一个标准化设置来比较不同的解决方案。挑战赛旨在解决多模态不完整性和多语言说话人识别问题，为研究者提供评估框架以推动相关技术进步。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验结果，但介绍了POLY-SIM 2026挑战赛的设立，该挑战赛将为多模态说话人识别系统在模态不完整和多语言环境下的性能评估提供基准平台。

Conclusion: 需要开发能够处理模态不完整性和多语言变异的鲁棒多模态说话人识别系统。POLY-SIM 2026挑战赛旨在推动这一领域的研究，通过标准化评估框架促进技术进步，提高系统在真实场景中的实用性。

Abstract: Multimodal speaker identification systems typically assume the availability of complete and homogeneous audio-visual modalities during both training and testing, and assume each speaker only speaks a single language. However, in real-world applications, such assumptions often do not hold. Visual or audio information may be missing due to occlusions, camera or microphone failures, or privacy constraints. Multilingual speakers introduce additional complexity due to linguistic variability across languages. These situations constitute substantial challenges for the robustness and generalization capabilities of multimodal speaker identification systems. Aim of the POLY-SIM 2026 challenge is to address these aspects of speaker identification and to provide a standardized setup for the comparison of the proposed solutions.

</details>


### [59] [Prospective clinical indication, post-hoc report leakage, and fusion design in multi-image chest radiograph classification: a patient-clustered evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13800)
*Kamran Shahid,Muhammad Munwar Iqbal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究比较了基于胸片图像、临床指征和多模态融合的不同模型在预测CheXbert衍生的报告观察指标方面的性能，发现前瞻性临床指征与报告目标强烈相关，排列感知融合具有竞争力，而回顾性报告文本会导致显著的标签循环问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片数据集通常包含在不同护理阶段产生的多个图像、临床指征、发现和印象。研究旨在评估这些不同输入对预测报告观察指标的影响，特别关注前瞻性临床指征的价值和多模态融合方法的效果。

Method: 使用15,000个ReXGradient-160K研究（包含两个可读图像和五个CheXbert衍生的报告观察指标）。采用冻结的DenseNet-121和Bio+ClinicalBERT编码器比较图像仅模型、指征仅模型、固定顺序多模态模型、随机交换模型、DeepSets模型和SectionGuard-MI模型。发现和印象仅作为回顾性泄漏控制进行评估。模型使用5个随机种子训练，通过2,000个患者簇自助法复制估计公共测试不确定性。

Result: 在U-Ones设置下，单一图像的宏观AUROC为0.643，双图像为0.694，临床指征为0.749，普通双图像加指征融合为0.780。SectionGuard-MI实现AUROC 0.783和AUPRC 0.260。相对于普通融合，其配对AUROC差异为0.0031（95% CI, -0.0042至0.0104），AUPRC差异为0.0289（95% CI, 0.0095至0.0413）。DeepSets具有最高前瞻性AUROC点估计值（0.787），随机交换融合具有最高前瞻性AUPRC点估计值（0.265）且校准优于SectionGuard-MI。完整报告文本单独达到AUROC 0.979和AUPRC 0.836。

Conclusion: 前瞻性临床指征与报告衍生的目标强烈相关，排列感知融合方法具有竞争力，回顾性报告文本导致显著的报告-标签循环问题，突显了在胸片分析中区分前瞻性和回顾性信息的重要性。

Abstract: Chest radiograph datasets often combine multiple images with Clinical Indication, Findings, and Impression, although these inputs are produced at different stages of care. We evaluated 15,000 ReXGradient-160K studies with two readable images and five CheXbert-derived report observations. Frozen DenseNet-121 and Bio+ClinicalBERT encoders were used to compare image-only, Indication-only, fixed-order multimodal, random-swap, DeepSets, and SectionGuard-MI models. Findings and Impression were evaluated only as post-hoc leakage controls. Models were trained with five seeds, and public-test uncertainty was estimated with 2,000 patient-cluster bootstrap replicates. Under U-Ones, macro AUROC was 0.643 for the primary image, 0.694 for two images, 0.749 for Indication, and 0.780 for ordinary two-image-plus-Indication fusion. SectionGuard-MI achieved AUROC 0.783 and AUPRC 0.260. Relative to ordinary fusion, its paired AUROC difference was 0.0031 (95% CI, -0.0042 to 0.0104; adjusted p=0.374), while its AUPRC difference was 0.0289 (95% CI, 0.0095 to 0.0413; adjusted p=0.004). DeepSets had the highest prospective AUROC point estimate (0.787), and random-swap fusion had the highest prospective AUPRC point estimate (0.265) with better calibration than SectionGuard-MI. Full report text alone reached AUROC 0.979 and AUPRC 0.836; AUROC remained above 0.973 after exact or expanded masking. These results show that prospective Indication is strongly associated with report-derived targets, permutation-aware fusion is competitive, and post-hoc report text creates substantial report-label circularity.

</details>


### [60] [WAVE-Stereo: Warp-Aligned Volume Encoding for Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13674)
*Zehan Liu,Yage He,Xianwu Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAVE-Stereo是一种新型立体匹配方法，通过并行融合相关体积和特征扭曲两种对应关系表示，并引入周期性全局上下文传播机制，在多个真实世界基准测试中实现了高精度和实时推理的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的迭代式立体匹配方法主要采用两种独立的对应关系表示：基于相关体积的显式匹配搜索和基于扭曲特征的局部残差细化。这两种表示方法被分开建模，缺乏统一的有效整合。

Method: 提出WAVE-Stereo方法，核心创新包括：1) GeoWarp对应编码器(GWCE)：在ConvGRU输入层并行编码匹配搜索、残差对齐和视差先验；2) 周期性全局上下文传播(PGCP)：通过周期性方式传播全局空间信息，以缓解纹理缺乏区域的匹配退化问题。

Result: 在Middlebury、ETH3D、KITTI 2012、KITTI 2015和Booster五个真实世界基准测试中，无需外部基础模型先验就实现了竞争力的零样本泛化精度：KITTI 2015上达到3.18% D1-all，Booster上达到4.42% Bad-2.0，同时保持66ms的实时推理速度。

Conclusion: WAVE-Stereo通过统一建模相关体积和特征扭曲的互补匹配线索，并引入全局上下文传播机制，在精度和效率之间取得了有利的平衡，为立体匹配任务提供了一种有效的新方法。

Abstract: Existing iterative stereo matching methods primarily adopt two types of correspondence representation: explicit matching search via correlation volumes and local residual refinement via warped features, yet the two remain separately modeled. We propose WAVE-Stereo, built on a core insight: correlation volumes and feature warping provide complementary matching cues. \textbf{GeoWarp Correspondence Encoder (GWCE)} encodes matching search, residual alignment, and disparity prior in parallel at the ConvGRU input. To mitigate matching degradation in textureless regions, we propose \textbf{Periodic Global Context Propagation (PGCP)}, which propagates global spatial information in a periodic manner. On five real-world benchmarks -- Middlebury, ETH3D, KITTI 2012, KITTI 2015, and Booster -- WAVE-Stereo achieves competitive zero-shot generalization accuracy without any external foundation model prior, achieving 3.18\% D1-all on KITTI 2015, 4.42\% Bad-2.0 on Booster, and 66ms real-time inference, striking a favorable balance between accuracy and efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/yamanoko-do/WAVE-Stereo.

</details>


### [61] [Multimodal Assessment of Pancreatic Cancer Resectability Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13826)
*Vincent Ochs,Christoph Kuemmerli,Florentin Bieder,Julia Wolleb,Joel L. Lavanchy,Julia Ruppel,Jan Liechti,Stephanie Taha-Mehlitz,Christian Andreas Nebiker,Beat Mueller,Giuseppe Kito Fusai,Joerg-Matthias Pollok,Anas Taha,Philippe C. Cattin,Sebastian Staubli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个全自动多模态深度学习框架，结合3D增强CT和结构化临床信息，自动分类胰腺导管腺癌的NCCN可切除性三类（可直接切除、临界可切除、局部晚期）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胰腺导管腺癌（PDAC）的可切除性评估主要依赖于CT成像中肿瘤与主要胰周血管的相互作用评估，但专家评估往往存在显著变异性，需要更准确、一致的评估方法

Method: 使用Swin-UNETR作为主干网络，通过辅助分割任务（胰腺、肿瘤和血管结构）获取解剖感知的图像表示；将这些特征与来自17个常规临床变量的紧凑临床嵌入融合，通过轻量级分类头进行处理；训练采用动态多任务目标，基于当前肿瘤Dice性能调整分割和分类之间的平衡

Result: 未在摘要中明确说明具体结果数据，但提出了一种能保持解剖信息和判别性的特征表示方法

Conclusion: 该全自动多模态深度学习框架能整合CT成像和临床信息，为PDAC患者的可切除性分类提供一种准确、一致的评估方法，有助于减少专家评估的变异性

Abstract: Accurate determination of pancreatic ductal adenocarcinoma (PDAC) resectability relies on evaluating how the tumor interacts with major peripancreatic vessels on CT imaging, yet expert assessment often shows substantial variability. We introduce a fully automated multimodal deep learning framework that jointly analyzes 3D contrast enhanced CT and structured clinical information to classify patients into the three National Comprehensive Cancer Network (NCCN) resectability categories (upfront resectable, borderline resectable, locally advanced). The approach uses a Swin-UNETR backbone to obtain anatomy aware image representations through auxiliary segmentation of pancreas, tumor, and vascular structures. These features are fused with a compact clinical embedding derived from 17 routinely collected variables and processed by a lightweight classification head. Model training is guided by a dynamic multitask objective that adapts the balance between segmentation and classification based on current tumor Dice performance, promoting feature representations that remain both anatomically informed and discriminative.

</details>


### [62] [Towards Spatial Supersensing in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13681)
*Tianjun Gu,Tianyu Xin,Kuan Zhang,Bowen Yang,Kok-Chung Chua,Peize Li,Xinran Zhang,Yupeng Chen,Qiyue Zhao,Qinlei Xie,Jianhang Liu,Yucheng Lu,Yinan Han,Marco Pavone,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了VSI-Super-Wild基准，用于评估模型在多样化真实场景中的长时空间超感知能力，揭示了模型在连贯世界状态追踪上的根本性缺陷，并提出了四种具体的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间超感知基准主要依赖由随机短视频拼接而成的合成长视频，且多局限于家庭场景，未能充分探索真实世界的连续性和多样性。为了弥补这一差距，需要构建一个大规模基准，用于评估模型在多样化真实场景中长时空间超感知的能力。

Method: 作者提出了VSI-Super-Wild基准，包含6,980个人工验证的问答对，源自442个真实世界视频，涵盖8个场景类别，其中包含超过4小时的长时记录。该基准系统地探测了世界状态的完整三元组：智能体（观察者）、对象（场景物品）和环境（地点与全局布局）。通过分析模型在该基准上的表现，作者量化了性能随世界状态复杂性和时间跨度的退化情况。

Result: 在VSI-Super-Wild基准上的评估结果显示，尽管静态图像理解有所进步，模型在需要随时间进行连贯世界状态追踪的任务上持续失败。性能随着世界状态复杂性和时间跨度的增加而下降。作者诊断出四种失败模式：空间坍塌、语义捷径、更新不足和实例混淆。

Conclusion: 当前模型在长时间跨度的空间超感知任务中表现不佳，根本原因在于缺乏将智能体、对象和环境绑定为统一空间世界模型的机制，这一差距指明了未来空间超感知研究的改进方向。

Abstract: Humans can efficiently parse continuous sensory streams, from hours to years, scaffolding an internal world model that grounds spatial reasoning and prediction. To mimic this capacity, spatial supersensing challenges multimodal models to move beyond linguistic understanding toward true world modeling. However, their benchmark relies on synthetic long videos, formed by concatenating random short clips, and is mostly limited to household scenes, leaving real-world continuity and diversity underexplored. To address the gap, we introduce $\textbf{VSI-Super-Wild}$, a large-scale benchmark for evaluating spatial supersensing over long temporal horizons in diverse in-the-wild scenes. Notably, inspired by cognitive studies on how humans structure experience, we systematically probe the full triad of world state: the agent (observer), objects (scene items), and the environment (places and global layout). In total, VSI-Super-Wild contains $\textbf{6,980}$ human-verified question-answer pairs derived from $\textbf{442}$ real-world videos spanning 8 scene categories, including long-form recordings exceeding 4 hours. Results on VSI-Super-Wild expose a fundamental disconnect: despite advances in static image understanding, models consistently fail at tasks that require coherent world-state tracking over time. We characterize how performance degrades with world-state complexity and temporal horizon, and diagnose four failure modes: spatial collapse, semantic shortcuts, insufficient update, and instance confusion. This taxonomy reveals that models lack mechanisms to bind objects, agents, and environments into a unified spatial world model, a fundamental gap that defines the path forward for spatial supersensing.

</details>


### [63] [Unleashing Multimodal Large Language Models for Training-free HOI Detection in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13881)
*Ting Lei,Jialin Liu,Zhu Xu,Yuxin Peng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgentHOI是一个无需训练的agentic框架，利用基础模型的多模态推理能力进行开放世界的人-物交互检测，通过上下文感知多轮推理和多方面交互定位机制解决不完整交互发现和模糊定位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的人-物交互检测方法依赖于预定义交互类别的监督学习，这限制了其在开放世界和组合场景中的泛化能力。现有的MLLM提示方法主要关注从预训练模型中提取判别性表征，而忽略了其固有的多模态推理能力，导致在模糊和开放世界交互场景中无法提供丰富的上下文推理。

Method: AgentHOI采用无需训练的方法，模块化地协调互补的视觉基础模块进行开放式的语义推理和空间定位。核心创新包括：1) 上下文感知多轮推理机制，逐步精炼交互假设以确保穷尽性和组合性的人-物交互发现；2) 多方面交互定位机制，通过生成整合语义、空间和外观线索的实例特定描述来增强定位精度。

Result: 大量实验表明，尽管不需要任何人-物交互检测数据训练，AgentHOI在真实世界设置中仍能实现优于最先进监督和弱监督方法的性能。

Conclusion: AgentHOI成功地将基础模型的通用多模态推理能力迁移到开放世界的人-物交互检测中，通过协调的模块化设计和创新推理机制，解决了现有方法在交互发现完整性和定位精度方面的局限性，为开放世界交互理解提供了一种新的无训练框架。

Abstract: Human-object interaction detection (HOID) has traditionally been formulated as a supervised detection problem over predefined interaction categories. While such paradigms achieve strong performance on closed-set benchmarks, they fundamentally entangle interaction understanding with dataset-specific supervision, limiting their ability to generalize to open-world and compositional scenarios. Recent HOI detectors attempt to leverage MLLMs through prompting strategies to transfer interaction-specific knowledge. However, such prompt-based approaches primarily focus on extracting discriminative representations from pretrained models, while underexploring their inherent multimodal reasoning capabilities. As a result, they struggle to provide informative contextual reasoning for ambiguous and open-world interaction scenarios. In this work, we present AgentHOI, a training-free, agentic framework that transfers the generalist multimodal reasoning capabilities of foundation models to HOI detection in the wild. Instead of learning interaction classifiers, AgentHOI modularly orchestrates complementary vision foundation modules to perform open-ended semantic reasoning and spatial grounding in a coordinated manner. To address the challenges of incomplete interaction discovery and ambiguous localization in complex scenes, we introduce two key mechanisms: (1) Context-aware Multi-round Reasoning, which progressively refines interaction hypotheses to ensure exhaustive and compositional HOI discovery, and (2) Multifaceted Interaction Localization, which enhances grounding precision by generating instance-specific descriptions that integrate semantic, spatial, and appearance cues. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AgentHOI achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art supervised and weakly supervised methods in real-world settings, despite requiring no HOID data for training.

</details>


### [64] [Calibrated Closed-Form Uncertainty for Radiative Gaussian Splatting in Sparse-View CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13682)
*Chulin Zhao,Yiran Xu,Shu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于变分密度后验的辐射高斯点云CT重建不确定性量化方法，通过闭式传播实现体素空间和投影空间的精确方差计算，并系统研究了其校准性能和应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辐射高斯点云稀疏视角CT重建方法仅输出点估计，缺乏对重建可信度的度量，无法量化不确定性，这限制了其在临床决策和自适应采样中的应用。

Method: 利用透射X射线成像的特性（投影和体素化对高斯点密度具有严格线性关系），为辐射高斯点配备变分密度后验，实现体素空间方差σ²(x)=∑ᵢ gᵢ(x)²sᵢ²和投影空间方差Var[Iₚ]=∑ᵢ wᵢₚ²sᵢ²的闭式精确传播。

Result: 在官方基准的15个场景中，该方法在14个场景上能有效排序真实重建误差（Spearman/AUSE/ECE指标），其中9个场景在单一温度缩放后达到幅度校准目标；而同期工作的扰动集合启发式方法移植到相同协议下表现不佳（秩相关性低至-0.08）。

Conclusion: 该方法首次实现了高斯点云CT重建的系统不确定性量化，校准后验能有效指导覆盖度门控的成熟度调度采集策略，并为剂量自适应停止规则提供理论基础，未来可进行实验验证。

Abstract: Radiative Gaussian splatting has made sparse-view CT reconstruction fast, but existing methods output point estimates with no notion of where the reconstruction can be trusted. We exploit a property of transmissive X-ray imaging that RGB splatting cannot claim -- projection and voxelization are strictly linear in the per-Gaussian densities -- to equip radiative Gaussians with a variational density posterior whose predictive variance propagates in closed form, exactly, in a single forward pass, in both volume space ($σ^2(x)=\sum_i g_i(x)^2 s_i^2$) and projection space ($\mathrm{Var}[I_p]=\sum_i w_{i,p}^2 s_i^2$). We present the first systematic calibration study for Gaussian-splatting CT (Spearman / AUSE / ECE with temperature scaling), showing that the resulting per-voxel uncertainty ranks true reconstruction error on 14 of 15 scenes of the official benchmark across three view budgets -- 9 of 15 additionally meeting our magnitude-calibration target after a single temperature -- while the perturbation-ensemble heuristic of concurrent work, transplanted to voxel space under the same protocol on our development scenes, does not (rank correlation as low as $-0.08$). We then dissect why uncalibrated acquisition scores can nevertheless select acceptable views, identifying three regimes -- flat (isotropic, balanced), pathological (degenerate coverage), and anisotropic -- and showing, in controlled single-scene testbeds, that principled uncertainty earns a measurable premium only in the last, motivating a coverage-gated, maturity-scheduled acquisition policy; the same calibrated posterior further points toward a dose-adaptive stopping rule, whose experimental validation we leave to future work.

</details>


### [65] [Music-to-Dance Generation via Atomic Movements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13978)
*Xinhao Cai,Yixuan Sun,Minghang Zheng,Qingchao Chen,Xin Jin,Song-chun Zhu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于原子运动的结构感知舞蹈生成框架，通过将舞蹈建模为可解释的原子运动序列来增强结构连贯性和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于神经网络的舞蹈生成方法通常将动作建模为连续信号，忽略了舞蹈的组合性质，导致生成的舞蹈结构不连贯且难以控制。

Method: 1. 构建原子运动词汇：对大规模舞蹈数据进行分割和聚类，使用大语言模型进行语义重标注，形成可解释的原子运动集合。
2. 两阶段生成框架：原子运动规划阶段预测原子运动的类型、时长和时机；完成阶段基于规划结构合成平滑连贯的动作。

Result: 实验表明，相比现有基线方法，该方法生成的舞蹈在结构连贯性、节奏对齐和感知自然度方面显著提升，同时通过显式结构表示增强了可解释性和可控编辑能力。

Conclusion: 将舞蹈建模为语义可解释的原子运动序列是提升音乐驱动舞蹈生成质量和可控性的有效方法，为结构化运动生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Music-driven dance generation aims to produce human motion that is both rhythmically synchronized and semantically consistent with music. While recent neural approaches have achieved impressive visual realism, they typically model motion as a continuous signal and neglect its compositional nature, making generated dances structurally incoherent and difficult to control. In this work, we introduce a structure-aware framework that models choreography as a sequence of atomic movements-semantically interpretable motion events that serve as the building blocks of dance. To construct this atomic movement vocabulary, we first segment large-scale dance data and cluster them into atomic movement groups. We then employ a large language model to semantically relabel and refine the clusters, yielding a set of interpretable and reusable atomic movements. Based on these atomic movement annotations, we design a two-stage generation framework that mirrors the human choreography process. In the atomic movement planning stage, the model predicts the type, duration, and timing of atomic movements conditioned on the input music, forming a symbolic dance allocation. In the completion stage, a transition-aware generator synthesizes smooth and stylistically coherent motion conditioned on the planned structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces dances with significantly improved structural coherence, rhythmic alignment, and perceptual naturalness compared to existing baselines, while providing enhanced interpretability and controllable editing through explicit structural representation.

</details>


### [66] [DNA: Dual-stage Native Attribution for Generated Image Source Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13685)
*Chao Wang,Kejiang Chen,Zijin Yang,Yaofei Wang,Yuang Qi,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双阶段原生溯源方法（DNA），用于对生成图像进行来源模型溯源，特别是在处理同一家族内相似变体模型时，通过分层架构信号实现粗到细的溯源，无需额外神经网络训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像生成技术的快速发展产生了许多同一家族内的变体模型，使得对可疑图像进行来源模型溯源在数字取证中越来越重要。现有主动方法依赖水印嵌入或模型修改，可能降低视觉质量并限制部署灵活性；被动方法通常依赖大规模监督训练或单一重建信号，在处理未知来源和区分高度相似的家族内变体方面能力有限。

Method: 提出双阶段原生溯源（DNA）框架：1）粗粒度阶段使用自编码器双重重建（AEDR）进行开放集家族级筛选；2）细粒度阶段使用原生预测一致性（NPC）进行封闭集模型级溯源，通过比较家族内变体在多个噪声水平下的原生预测误差（在语义条件下），并使用归一化校准分数进行来源归属。该方法基于观察到潜在生成模型中的溯源信号在架构层次上自然分层：VAE级线索反映家族共享信息，而骨干网络级线索捕获变体特定行为。

Result: 构建了DNA-30K基准数据集，包含6个家族24个候选模型生成的30,000张图像，以及非候选生成图像和自然图像作为未知来源。实验显示DNA在随机猜测准确率低于1%的任务中实现了89.11%的端到端溯源准确率，即使在AEDR作为粗粒度阶段的情况下，也比最强基线提高了33.81%。

Conclusion: 提出的DNA框架通过利用生成模型中天然存在的分层溯源信号，实现了高效且准确的图像来源模型溯源，特别是在处理同一家族内相似变体时表现出色，为数字取证提供了有效的被动溯源解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of image generation has produced numerous within-family variants, making source-model attribution of suspect images increasingly important for digital forensics. Existing proactive methods rely on watermark embedding or model modification, which may degrade visual quality and limit deployment flexibility. Passive methods often rely on large-scale supervised training or a single reconstruction signal, limiting their ability to handle unknown sources and distinguish highly similar within-family variants. We observe that attribution signals in latent generative models are naturally stratified across architectural levels: VAE-level cues reflect family-shared information, whereas backbone-level cues capture variant-specific behaviors. Motivated by this insight, we propose Dual-stage Native Attribution (DNA), a coarse-to-fine framework that follows this hierarchy without additional neural-network training. The coarse-grained stage uses Autoencoder Double-Reconstruction (AEDR) for efficient open-set family-level screening. The fine-grained stage performs closed-set model-level attribution with Native Prediction Consistency (NPC), which compares native prediction errors of within-family variants across multiple noise levels under semantic conditioning and attributes the source via normalized calibrated scores. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct DNA-30K, a benchmark for within-family variant attribution under open-set family-level evaluation. It comprises 30,000 images generated by 24 candidate models across six families spanning both denoising diffusion and flow matching, plus non-candidate generated and natural images as unknown sources. Experiments show that DNA achieves 89.11% end-to-end attribution accuracy on a task where random guessing accuracy is below 1% and outperforms the strongest baseline by 33.81% even when AEDR is used as the coarse-grained stage.

</details>


### [67] [Multi-Expert Routing for Multi-Domain Low-Resource OCR: A Manchu Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14041)
*Zhan Chen,Jiqiao Ma,Chih-wen Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个历史满文OCR的多专家系统，通过重用迭代微调检查点作为领域专家，并使用轻量级页面级图像分类器根据视觉风格调度页面。当检查点池缺乏合适的专家时，为特定领域训练额外专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史满文OCR需要处理多种视觉上不同的书写风格（楷书、行书和奏折所用的半草体），但标注数据有限。传统单一模型难以适应这些不同的视觉域，需要一种能够有效利用有限标注数据的方法。

Method: 采用多专家系统，重用迭代微调过程的检查点作为领域专家，构建检查点池。使用轻量级页面级图像分类器根据视觉风格将页面分发到合适的专家。如果检查点池缺乏合适的专家，则为该领域训练额外专家。系统通过路由器实现页面级域分类。

Result: 在三个固定的测试集上，路由系统在每种书写风格上都达到了与选定专家相同的两位小数精度：楷书0.30%字符错误率、奏折1.57%、行书4.83%。路由器实现了99.3%的页面级域分类准确率，与域标签预言机达到相同的精度。三个选定专家中有两个并非专门为其最终领域训练的。

Conclusion: 提出的多专家系统通过重用微调检查点和轻量级路由器，能够有效处理历史满文的不同书写风格，在有限标注数据下取得了良好的OCR性能。系统设计具有可复现性，为多域OCR问题提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Historical Manchu OCR must accommodate various visually distinct writing styles, including regular script, running script, and the semi-cursive chancery hand used in palace memorials, despite limited labeled data. We study a multi-expert system that reuses checkpoints from an iterative fine-tuning process as domain specialists and uses a lightweight page-level image classifier to dispatch pages by visual style. When the checkpoint pool lacks a suitable specialist, we train an additional expert for that domain. On three frozen test sets, the routed system matches the selected specialist for each style at two-decimal precision: 0.30 percent CER on regular script, 1.57 percent on memorials, and 4.83 percent on running script. The router achieves 99.3 percent page-level domain accuracy and matches the domain-label oracle at the same precision. Two of the three selected specialists were not trained specifically for their final domain; only the running-script expert was trained with that domain as its target. We report the evaluation protocol, router design, and per-page predictions to make the comparison reproducible.

</details>


### [68] [EgoProceVQA: A Novel Egocentric Procedural Understanding Task with Self-Skill-Exploration Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13792)
*Junlong Li,Junxi Li,Yuxiang Yang,Wenbin Zou,Lap-Pui Chau,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoProceVQA任务以评估第一人称视角程序性推理能力，开发了EgoProceGen数据生成平台构建基准，并设计了EgoProceAgent自技能探索代理框架，显著提升了模型在程序性理解任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的第一人称视角视频理解评估很少涉及程序性理解，并且在广泛使用的多模态大语言模型视频问答范式中，往往忽视了复杂的关键步骤级推理能力。这种能力对于构建可部署在可穿戴设备上的程序性AI助手至关重要。为了填补这一空白，需要系统性地评估当前多模态大语言模型和代理在第一人称视角程序性推理方面的能力。

Method: 本文首先提出了第一人称视角程序性理解视频问答任务（EgoProceVQA），并开发了EgoProceGen数据生成平台，用于高效构建针对六种问题类型的问答数据。基于该平台，构建了包含3,600个问题、四个常见程序场景和31个日常程序任务的基准。进一步提出了EgoProceAgent，一个自技能探索的代理框架，设计了用于程序性理解的通用工具库和跨工具与模型共享的标准化子技能库，使代理能够在无真实监督的情况下进行自我探索，通过组合和选择子技能来发现有效的技能策略。

Result: 在EgoProceVQA上的评估表明，现有的多模态大语言模型和代理在程序性理解方面仍有显著的改进空间。提出的EgoProceAgent框架通过自技能探索，能够在多个任务上达到开源模型中的最先进性能。该框架通过探索如何组合和选择子技能，为不同问题发现了有效的技能策略。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入EgoProceVQA任务、构建EgoProceGen数据生成平台和提出EgoProceAgent自技能探索代理框架，为第一人称视角程序性理解建立了一个统一的基础，显著提升了模型在程序性推理任务上的表现，并为未来研究提供了基准和工具。

Abstract: Most daily activities are inherently procedural. However, existing evaluations for egocentric video understanding seldom address procedural understanding and largely overlook complex key-step-level reasoning under the widely used video question answering (VQA) paradigm for MLLMs. Such capabilities are crucial for building procedural AI assistants deployable on wearable devices. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Egocentric Procedural Understanding VQA task (EgoProceVQA), which systematically evaluates egocentric procedural reasoning abilities of current MLLMs and agents through six types of key-step-centric questions. Furthermore, we develop EgoProceGen, a data generation platform that efficiently constructs QA data tailored to different question types. Based on this platform, we build a benchmark with 3,600 questions, four common procedural scenarios, and 31 everyday procedural tasks. Evaluations on EgoProceVQA show that existing MLLMs and agents still have substantial room for improvement in procedural understanding. Therefore, we further propose EgoProceAgent, a self-skill-exploration agentic framework. We design a generic tool library for procedural understanding and a standardized sub-skill library shared across tools and models, enabling self-exploration without ground-truth supervision. By exploring how to compose and select sub-skills, the agent discovers effective skill strategies for diverse problems, and attains state-of-the-art performance among open-source models on multiple tasks. Together, our benchmark, generation platform, and agentic framework establish a unified foundation for EgoProceVQA. Project page: https://z1oong.github.io/EgoProceVQA/.

</details>


### [69] [RainDancer: RGB-Event Video Deraining with Rain-Oriented Spiking Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13802)
*Kui Jiang,Runzhe Li,Zhaocheng Yu,Guanglu Sun,Junjun Jiang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RainDancer：基于分解-交互范式的渐进式RGB-事件视频去雨框架，通过模态内分解雨滴和背景组件后进行跨模态交互，实现高质量去雨效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频去雨方法主要依赖RGB序列和时间冗余，但在动态雨景中，雨痕、纹理、边界、运动和遮挡可能共享相似的视觉模式，导致RGB-only恢复存在歧义。事件相机提供高时间分辨率的互补运动敏感线索，但事件流包含传感器噪声和背景触发响应，直接RGB-事件融合可能引入跨模态干扰。

Method: 提出基于分解-交互范式的渐进式RGB-事件视频去雨框架RainDancer。核心思想是在跨模态交互之前分离每个模态内的雨滴和背景组件。RGB分支中，将帧特征渐进分解为雨滴和背景表示；事件分支中，使用面向雨滴的脉冲神经网络模块捕捉与雨运动相关的稀疏突发事件动态。然后在语义对齐的表示之间执行组件级融合以实现结构保留和雨滴抑制。进一步引入事件域监督来正则化稀疏事件重建、结构一致性和梯度方向。
